{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"Design a \"peak-innovative\" workstream for SIGIL (a local-first, offense-free, provable personal AI orchestrator at /home/kali/sigil): an advanced INTERNET SCRAPER + web-research capability that fetches PUBLIC content, grounds every claim to its verbatim source, respects robots.txt + rate limits, scope-gates its targets, and feeds cited knowledge into the memory spine. \"Scrape the internet and way more\" — a future-grade grounded research engine. Strict doctrine: offense-free; public content only; correlatable (NOT evasive) — reuse the existing stable User-Agent; throttle; third parties are never attacked (CLAUDE.md §II/§VI, SIGIL.md §4.8/§12); prove-don't-guess / serve-the-quote (every served fact is a verbatim source span, cited to the spine; a model's paraphrase is advisory-only). Phase cadence: reuse-first → deterministic tests → real proof → red-pen → fix → PR.\n\nSUBSTRATE (use this; do NOT re-explore):\n- FETCH (complete, reuse verbatim): sigil/agents/sources.py read_source(ref, timeout=20, max_chars=20000) — SSRF-gated: _vetted_ip resolves once + verifies EVERY resolved address is public unicast (rejects private/loopback/link-local/reserved/multicast/IPv4-mapped) + PINS the IP (_PinnedHTTPConnection/_PinnedHTTPSConnection keep SNI+cert on the original host), _NoRedirect refuses all 3xx, _strip_html strips script/style/tags; NEVER raises → \"\" on failure/refusal. Stable correlatable UA \"SIGIL-SCHOLAR/1.0 (authorized owner research)\". is_public_host(host). CAVEAT: NO rate-limiting, NO robots.txt, NO per-host throttle — only timeout + max_chars.\n- GROUNDING (serve-the-quote, reuse): sigil/agents/scholar.py grounds_in_source(quote, source_text) = verbatim substring AND ≥2 salient tokens; compose_report SERVES the verbatim source span (the model's claim is advisory-only); Synthesizer Protocol + ClaudeSynthesizer (claude -p → JSON claims w/ verbatim quotes) + a deterministic double; Scholar agent (name=\"SCHOLAR\", ceiling A1). consolidate/gate.py salient() tokenizer + _real_span verbatim-span extractor (reuse for grounding scraped claims).\n- SCOPE + THROTTLE primitives: sigil/agents/operator_scope.py OperatorScope (realpath allowlist, empty=deny-all) is the model for a per-host/domain scrape ALLOWLIST. sigil/governor/budget.py BudgetLedger/BudgetCaps (coarse per-agent per-UTC-day record count, opt-in ~/.sigil/budgets.json, fail-closed in Governor.decide) is the only budget primitive — NO per-host request-rate limiter exists (must be built). Correlatable UA already present.\n- MEMORY FEED: the spine (sigil/spine/store.py append, hash-chained) + the KINDS registry (models.py) + how SCHOLAR writes a `report` record grounded in sources; sigil/consolidate for entity/graph promotion. A scraped, grounded claim should become a cited spine record (recallable via the existing memory/MCP tools).\n- WARDEN: kernel_classify + tiers.rs — http.get=A0 but web.fetch/scrape.fetch=A3 (fail-closed). SCHOLAR ceiling A1 (research never touches external state).\n\nDesign a DETAILED plan. Produce:\n1. PEAK-INNOVATIVE mechanisms — advanced, not just \"loop read_source\": (a) a bounded CRAWL FRONTIER (VOI/priority BFS over public pages, depth+page caps, scope-gated to an owner-authorized domain allowlist like OperatorScope) reusing read_source per fetch; (b) a real POLITENESS layer — per-host RATE LIMITER (token-bucket / min-interval, built new) + robots.txt fetch+parse+RESPECT (doctrine: respect, never evade) + the correlatable UA + backoff on 429/503; (c) STRUCTURED extraction — beyond HTML-strip: tables, links, entities, and JS-page rendering via the WS-F headless-browser provider (shared) when a page needs it; (d) GROUNDED research orchestration — a scraped claim is GROUNDED only if its quote is verbatim in the fetched page (reuse grounds_in_source/_real_span), served with its citation, then written as a cited `report`/knowledge record into the memory spine (so scraped knowledge is recallable + provenance-linked, never a fabrication); (e) a research ORCHESTRATOR extending SCHOLAR: given a question, fan out over public sources, ground every claim, synthesize a cited report into memory (mirror the deep-research pattern).\n2. Ordered, independently-mergeable SLICES naming files to create/modify + exact reuse points.\n3. Test + real-proof strategy (deterministic: a LOCAL test site with known pages/robots.txt; assert robots is respected; assert the per-host rate limiter enforces min-interval; assert a private/loopback URL is refused by the SSRF gate; assert every served claim is verbatim-grounded + cited; a real public-fetch proof skip-if-offline) + red-pen lenses (SSRF/rebinding via the crawl frontier; robots ignored; rate limit bypassable; a fabricated/ungrounded claim served as fact; scope-gate escape to a non-allowlisted host; the scraper becoming recon/attack tooling; PII/credential harvesting).\n4. Doctrine-safety analysis (public-only, correlatable, throttled, scope-gated, serve-the-quote, never-attack) + abuse vectors a reviewer will hunt.\n5. How \"way more\" is bounded to stay offense-free (what it will NOT do).\nDo not write code; design + enumerate. Return a thorough structured document."},"uuid":"434b3bca-afd3-4b48-bca0-405943cf67ed","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:09.837Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"9df7a9fe-ec16-4835-9d2c-2fc01fd8036f","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- crucible: How to operate CRUCIBLE — the authorized, prove-don't-guess offensive-security engine in framework/v2. Read this BEFORE running an engagement/scan, re-verifying a finding, inspecting the event spine, reasoning about the veracity/anti-hallucination layer, the learning/metacognition core, or the safety stack. Use it when a task names CRUCIBLE, OBSIDIAN, `python3 -m framework.v2 <subcommand>`, findings/oracles/evidence certificates, the blackboard event spine, critics, reflection, or the reward/calibration loop.\n- deep-research: Deep research harness — fan-out web searches, fetch sources, adversarially verify claims, synthesize a cited report. - When the user wants a deep, multi-source, fact-checked research report on any topic. BEFORE invoking, check if the question is specific enough to research directly — if underspecified (e.g., \"what car to buy\" without budget/use-case/region), ask 2-3 clarifying questions to narrow scope. Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- adversarial-sweep: Independent multi-lens adversarial re-attack of a defensive-oracle domain — the FORGE stage-9 companion to RED-PEN. Attack from disjoint lenses, refute each objection with a real repro, report only what survives. - Before merging any AEGIS/FORGE domain stream, alongside (not instead of) the red-pen agent. Pass the domain spec via args. On the email-auth stream it caught 3 CRITICAL/LOW defects PAST a RED-PEN PASS — near-zero-FP cannot be self-certified by one reviewer.\n- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".\n- artifact-design: Design guidance and fundamentals for Artifacts.\n- artifact-capabilities: Runtime capabilities a published Artifact page can be granted — behavior static HTML cannot provide on its own, such as the page reading live or connected data, keeping state shared across viewers, or updating and republishing itself. Serves this user's live capability roster and the typed call definitions. Load it whenever the user asks for an artifact needing any such runtime behavior.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by exercising it end-to-end and observing behavior — drive the affected flow, not just tests or typecheck. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. Don't invoke it on a diff that only touches tests, docs, or other code with no runtime surface to drive (a change to product source always has one) — there's nothing to observe.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled cloud agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring cloud agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a GitHub pull request; for your working diff use /code-review\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch","skillCount":19,"isInitial":true,"names":["crucible","deep-research","adversarial-sweep","dataviz","artifact-design","artifact-capabilities","update-config","keybindings-help","verify","code-review","simplify","fewer-permission-prompts","loop","schedule","claude-api","run","init","review","security-review"]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"ee1577ba-ecfc-482a-a670-92bf0df4f004","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:09.844Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"ff81c4f6-981c-42fd-b261-2aba1d8a3146","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01RDncYdwoeUZC8DhaUvmHh1","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Source reading for SCHOLAR — a URL (web fetch, HTML stripped) or a local file/doc path → text.\n2\tKept small + dependency-free (urllib + a crude tag strip); a richer extractor is a later add.\n3\t\n4\tSSRF GATE (Phase 6, closes the carried debt + red-pen SSRF-REBIND-TOCTOU): the host is resolved\n5\tONCE; every resolved address must be public (not private/loopback/link-local/reserved/multicast/\n6\tunspecified — IPv4 and IPv6, incl. IPv4-mapped IPv6); and the connection is then PINNED to that exact\n7\tvetted IP, so a rebinding DNS that flips to an internal address between the check and the fetch cannot\n8\tsteer SCHOLAR onto `169.254.169.254`/`localhost`/an internal service. Redirects are not followed. A\n9\trefused/unreadable source is simply empty evidence, never a fabricated one.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport http.client\n13\timport ipaddress\n14\timport re\n15\timport socket\n16\timport urllib.request\n17\tfrom pathlib import Path\n18\tfrom typing import Optional\n19\tfrom urllib.parse import urlsplit\n20\t\n21\t_TAG = re.compile(r\"<[^>]+>\")\n22\t_WS = re.compile(r\"\\s+\")\n23\t_SCRIPT = re.compile(r\"<(script|style)[^>]*>.*?</\\1>\", re.S | re.I)\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tdef _strip_html(html: str) -> str:\n27\t    html = _SCRIPT.sub(\" \", html)\n28\t    return _WS.sub(\" \", _TAG.sub(\" \", html)).strip()\n29\t\n30\t\n31\tdef _vetted_ip(host: str) -> Optional[str]:\n32\t    \"\"\"Resolve `host`; return ONE address to pin iff EVERY resolved address is public unicast, else\n33\t    None. Fail-closed: an unresolvable host, or any private/loopback/link-local/reserved/multicast/\n34\t    unspecified/IPv4-mapped-internal address, yields None.\"\"\"\n35\t    if not host:\n36\t        return None\n37\t    try:\n38\t        infos = socket.getaddrinfo(host, None)\n39\t    except (socket.gaierror, UnicodeError, OSError):\n40\t        return None\n41\t    if not infos:\n42\t        return None\n43\t    chosen = None\n44\t    for family, _t, _p, _c, sockaddr in infos:\n45\t        try:\n46\t            ip = ipaddress.ip_address(sockaddr[0])\n47\t        except ValueError:\n48\t            return None\n49\t        # unwrap IPv4-mapped/compat IPv6 (::ffff:127.0.0.1) so an internal v4 can't hide as v6\n50\t        if isinstance(ip, ipaddress.IPv6Address) and ip.ipv4_mapped is not None:\n51\t            ip = ip.ipv4_mapped\n52\t        if (ip.is_private or ip.is_loopback or ip.is_link_local or ip.is_reserved\n53\t                or ip.is_multicast or ip.is_unspecified):\n54\t            return None\n55\t        if chosen is None:\n56\t            chosen = sockaddr[0]\n57\t    return chosen\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef is_public_host(host: str) -> bool:\n61\t    return _vetted_ip(host) is not None\n62\t\n63\t\n64\tclass _PinnedHTTPConnection(http.client.HTTPConnection):\n65\t    pinned_ip: Optional[str] = None\n66\t\n67\t    def connect(self):\n68\t        self.sock = socket.create_connection((self.pinned_ip or self.host, self.port),\n69\t                                              self.timeout, self.source_address)\n70\t        if getattr(self, \"_tunnel_host\", None):\n71\t            self._tunnel()\n72\t\n73\t\n74\tclass _PinnedHTTPSConnection(http.client.HTTPSConnection):\n75\t    pinned_ip: Optional[str] = None\n76\t\n77\t    def connect(self):\n78\t        sock = socket.create_connection((self.pinned_ip or self.host, self.port),\n79\t                                        self.timeout, self.source_address)\n80\t        if getattr(self, \"_tunnel_host\", None):\n81\t            self.sock = sock\n82\t            self._tunnel()\n83\t            sock = self.sock\n84\t        # SNI + certificate validation still use the ORIGINAL hostname, not the pinned IP.\n85\t        self.sock = self._context.wrap_socket(sock, server_hostname=self.host)\n86\t\n87\t\n88\tclass _PinnedHTTPHandler(urllib.request.HTTPHandler):\n89\t    def __init__(self, ip):\n90\t        super().__init__()\n91\t        self._ip = ip\n92\t\n93\t    def http_open(self, req):\n94\t        ip = self._ip\n95\t\n96\t        def factory(host, **kw):\n97\t            c = _PinnedHTTPConnection(host, **kw)\n98\t            c.pinned_ip = ip\n99\t            return c\n100\t        return self.do_open(factory, req)\n101\t\n102\t\n103\tclass _PinnedHTTPSHandler(urllib.request.HTTPSHandler):\n104\t    def __init__(self, ip):\n105\t        super().__init__()\n106\t        self._ip = ip\n107\t\n108\t    def https_open(self, req):\n109\t        ip = self._ip\n110\t\n111\t        def factory(host, **kw):\n112\t            c = _PinnedHTTPSConnection(host, **kw)\n113\t            c.pinned_ip = ip\n114\t            return c\n115\t        return self.do_open(factory, req, context=self._context)\n116\t\n117\t\n118\tclass _NoRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):\n119\t    \"\"\"Refuse to follow 3xx — a public URL must not redirect SCHOLAR onto an internal host.\"\"\"\n120\t    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):\n121\t        return None\n122\t\n123\t\n124\tdef read_source(ref: str, *, timeout: int = 20, max_chars: int = 20000) -> str:\n125\t    \"\"\"Return the text of a source. `http(s)://…` is SSRF-gated (resolve-once, verify-all-public,\n126\t    pin the IP, no redirects) then fetched + HTML-stripped; anything else is a local path. Never\n127\t    raises — returns '' on failure or refusal.\"\"\"\n128\t    try:\n129\t        if ref.startswith((\"http://\", \"https://\")):\n130\t            host = urlsplit(ref).hostname or \"\"\n131\t            ip = _vetted_ip(host)\n132\t            if ip is None:\n133\t                return \"\"       # SSRF gate: non-public / unresolvable → no request leaves the machine\n134\t            opener = urllib.request.build_opener(_PinnedHTTPHandler(ip), _PinnedHTTPSHandler(ip), _NoRedirect)\n135\t            req = urllib.request.Request(ref, headers={\"User-Agent\": \"SIGIL-SCHOLAR/1.0 (authorized owner research)\"})\n136\t            with opener.open(req, timeout=timeout) as r:\n137\t                raw = r.read().decode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\")\n138\t            return _strip_html(raw)[:max_chars]\n139\t        return Path(ref).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"ignore\")[:max_chars]\n140\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — an unreadable source is simply empty evidence\n141\t        return \"\"\n142\t"}]},"uuid":"f80554dc-3c3b-4b07-b2b8-d34b526d1ef0","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:43.682Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ff81c4f6-981c-42fd-b261-2aba1d8a3146","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"09ffb46a-2aae-47db-87ed-ebe3c5fe2bc1","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAA4Q9PHay62cUtwVyUq","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013Czf25THL3K7X5pmp8pTZJ","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/kernel_classify.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1397,"cache_read_input_tokens":12207,"output_tokens":288,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1397},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":288,"cache_read_input_tokens":12207,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1397,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1397,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAA4P2uY26Km4ntG6gR8","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"5a5d4fa9-0318-4683-aad1-1dedaa2cca48","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:44.367Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"09ffb46a-2aae-47db-87ed-ebe3c5fe2bc1","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013LDvYeiYXP7PjV84o32KWE","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"OperatorScope (Phase 7, WS-B B-ii) — the capability-scoped two-ring sandbox that bounds where the\n2\tOperator may read and auto-write. Wraps the currently-UNGUARDED local-path branch of\n3\t`sources.read_source` so the Operator can never touch an arbitrary path.\n4\t\n5\tTwo rings:\n6\t  • read_roots — the Operator may READ here (A0/A1).\n7\t  • auto_write_roots — the narrower set where an A1 reversible write may AUTO-apply; a write/delete\n8\t    inside a read root but OUTSIDE an auto-write root must be QUEUED for approval, not auto-run.\n9\t\n10\tEvery path is realpath-resolved (symlinks followed) and must be *within* an allowed root — a `..`,\n11\ta symlink escaping the root, or an absolute path elsewhere resolves outside and is refused. EMPTY\n12\troots = deny-all (fail-closed), the inverse of `read_source`'s current unguarded default. The\n13\tresolved real path is returned so the caller opens exactly what was vetted (no re-resolve → no\n14\tTOCTOU gap between the check and the open).\"\"\"\n15\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n16\t\n17\tfrom pathlib import Path\n18\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef _within(p: Path, root: Path) -> bool:\n22\t    return p == root or p.is_relative_to(root)\n23\t\n24\t\n25\tclass OperatorScope:\n26\t    def __init__(self, read_roots: Optional[List[str]] = None,\n27\t                 auto_write_roots: Optional[List[str]] = None):\n28\t        self.read_roots = [Path(r).resolve() for r in (read_roots or [])]\n29\t        self.auto_write_roots = [Path(r).resolve() for r in (auto_write_roots or [])]\n30\t\n31\t    @staticmethod\n32\t    def _realpath(path: str) -> Optional[Path]:\n33\t        \"\"\"Resolve symlinks + `..` (strict=False so a not-yet-existing write target still resolves\n34\t        via its existing parents). None on an unresolvable path.\"\"\"\n35\t        try:\n36\t            return Path(path).resolve()\n37\t        except (OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError):\n38\t            return None\n39\t\n40\t    def resolve(self, path: str, need: str = \"read\") -> Optional[Path]:\n41\t        \"\"\"Return the vetted REAL path iff it is within the ring for `need` ('read' | 'write'), else\n42\t        None. Open THIS returned path (never re-resolve `path`) to avoid a check/open TOCTOU.\"\"\"\n43\t        rp = self._realpath(path)\n44\t        if rp is None:\n45\t            return None\n46\t        roots = self.auto_write_roots if need == \"write\" else self.read_roots\n47\t        return rp if any(_within(rp, root) for root in roots) else None\n48\t\n49\t    def in_read(self, path: str) -> bool:\n50\t        return self.resolve(path, \"read\") is not None\n51\t\n52\t    def in_auto_write(self, path: str) -> bool:\n53\t        return self.resolve(path, \"write\") is not None\n54\t"}]},"uuid":"6680bd40-253d-4ef4-a962-a1c3880d622b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:44.400Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"09ffb46a-2aae-47db-87ed-ebe3c5fe2bc1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"9c9aac92-d72f-4505-85fb-96acf92a15fc","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01X1oLWrMQzKwzf76MnNK8Ef","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SCHOLAR (SIGIL §4.5) — research & analysis: long-horizon research, sourced synthesis. Ceiling\n2\tA1 (research never touches external state). Its focus is EPISTEMICS: every claim carries a\n3\tsource and a verbatim quote, confidence is explicit, and a claim that does NOT verify against its\n4\tcited source is DEMOTED (not asserted) — the same serve-the-quote gate that governs the\n5\tconsolidation, reused here. The output is a `report` record grounded in real sources, so future\n6\trecall of the research is cited and honest, not confident. The synthesizer is pluggable\n7\t(`ClaudeSynthesizer` via `claude -p`, or a deterministic double).\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport json\n11\timport re\n12\timport subprocess\n13\tfrom typing import Dict, List, Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n14\t\n15\tfrom ..consolidate.gate import salient   # reuse the veracity tokenizer\n16\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n17\tfrom .sources import read_source\n18\t\n19\t_WS = re.compile(r\"\\s+\")\n20\t_MIN_QUOTE_SALIENT = 2\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tdef _norm(t: str) -> str:\n24\t    return _WS.sub(\" \", (t or \"\")).strip().lower()\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef grounds_in_source(quote: str, source_text: str) -> bool:\n28\t    \"\"\"A claim's quote must be verbatim in the cited source AND specific (≥2 salient tokens) —\n29\t    exactly the serve-the-quote discipline, applied to research sources.\"\"\"\n30\t    q = _norm(quote)\n31\t    if not q or len(salient(quote)) < _MIN_QUOTE_SALIENT:\n32\t        return False\n33\t    return q in _norm(source_text)\n34\t\n35\t\n36\t@runtime_checkable\n37\tclass Synthesizer(Protocol):\n38\t    def synthesize(self, question: str, docs: Dict[str, str]) -> List[dict]: ...  # [{claim,source,quote,confidence}]\n39\t\n40\t\n41\tclass ClaudeSynthesizer:\n42\t    \"\"\"`claude -p` over the sources → JSON claims, each citing a source ref + a verbatim quote.\"\"\"\n43\t    def __init__(self, claude_bin: str = \"/home/kali/.local/bin/claude\",\n44\t                 model: str = \"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001\", timeout: int = 180):\n45\t        self.claude_bin, self.model, self.timeout = claude_bin, model, timeout\n46\t\n47\t    def synthesize(self, question: str, docs: Dict[str, str]) -> List[dict]:\n48\t        rendered = \"\\n\\n\".join(f\"[SOURCE {ref}]\\n{text[:4000]}\" for ref, text in docs.items())\n49\t        prompt = (\n50\t            \"You are a research analyst. Answer the QUESTION using ONLY the SOURCES. For every claim, \"\n51\t            \"cite the exact source ref and copy a VERBATIM quote from it that supports the claim; if a \"\n52\t            \"source does not support a claim, do not make it. If sources DISAGREE, report both. Return \"\n53\t            'ONLY a JSON array: [{\"claim\":\"...\",\"source\":\"<ref>\",\"quote\":\"<verbatim>\",\"confidence\":<0..1>}].'\n54\t            f\"\\n\\nQUESTION: {question}\\n\\nSOURCES:\\n{rendered}\\n\"\n55\t        )\n56\t        try:\n57\t            proc = subprocess.run([self.claude_bin, \"-p\", prompt, \"--model\", self.model],\n58\t                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n59\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n60\t            return []\n61\t        m = re.search(r\"\\[.*\\]\", proc.stdout, re.S)\n62\t        if not m:\n63\t            return []\n64\t        try:\n65\t            items = json.loads(m.group(0))\n66\t        except json.JSONDecodeError:\n67\t            return []\n68\t        out = []\n69\t        for it in items if isinstance(items, list) else []:\n70\t            if isinstance(it, dict) and it.get(\"claim\") and it.get(\"source\"):\n71\t                out.append({\"claim\": str(it[\"claim\"]), \"source\": str(it[\"source\"]),\n72\t                            \"quote\": str(it.get(\"quote\", \"\")), \"confidence\": float(it.get(\"confidence\", 0.5) or 0.5)})\n73\t        return out\n74\t\n75\t\n76\tdef compose_report(question: str, graded: List[dict], sources: List[str]) -> str:\n77\t    grounded = [g for g in graded if g[\"grounded\"]]\n78\t    lines = [f\"# SCHOLAR research — {question}\", \"\",\n79\t             f\"Sources consulted: {len(sources)}. Claims: {len(graded)} \"\n80\t             f\"({len(grounded)} source-verified, {len(graded) - len(grounded)} unverified).\", \"\"]\n81\t    if grounded:\n82\t        # SERVE THE QUOTE, not the model's claim: the authoritative content is the verbatim source\n83\t        # span (which is what actually verified). The model's claim is advisory ONLY — a fabricated\n84\t        # claim paired with a real quote must NOT be presented as a source-verified fact.\n85\t        lines.append(\"## Source-verified spans (the authoritative evidence, verbatim)\")\n86\t        for g in sorted(grounded, key=lambda x: -x[\"confidence\"]):\n87\t            lines.append(f\"- \\\"{g['quote'][:200]}\\\"\")\n88\t            lines.append(f\"    — {g['source']}  ·  model's reading (ADVISORY, not verified): \"\n89\t                         f\"{g['claim'][:120]}  [{g['confidence']:.0%}]\")\n90\t    ungrounded = [g for g in graded if not g[\"grounded\"]]\n91\t    if ungrounded:\n92\t        lines.append(\"\\n## Unverified (the model asserted these but no verbatim source span backs them — NOT relied upon)\")\n93\t        for g in ungrounded:\n94\t            lines.append(f\"- {g['claim'][:120]}  (claimed source {g['source']})\")\n95\t    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n96\t\n97\t\n98\tclass Scholar(Agent):\n99\t    name = \"SCHOLAR\"\n100\t    mandate = \"sourced research; claims carry sources + confidence; disagreement reported, not averaged\"\n101\t    ceiling = Tier.A1\n102\t\n103\t    def run(self, question: str, sources: List[str], *, synthesizer: Optional[Synthesizer] = None) -> AgentResult:\n104\t        docs = {ref: read_source(ref) for ref in sources}\n105\t        claims = (synthesizer or ClaudeSynthesizer()).synthesize(question, docs)\n106\t        graded = [{**c, \"grounded\": grounds_in_source(c.get(\"quote\", \"\"), docs.get(c[\"source\"], \"\"))}\n107\t                  for c in claims]\n108\t        text = compose_report(question, graded, sources)\n109\t        g = sum(1 for x in graded if x[\"grounded\"])\n110\t        res = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"report\", {\n111\t            \"subject\": question, \"text\": text, \"sources\": sources,\n112\t            \"claims\": len(graded), \"grounded\": g}, Tier.A1)])\n113\t        res.notes.append(f\"researched '{question[:50]}' over {len(sources)} source(s): \"\n114\t                         f\"{g}/{len(graded)} claims source-verified\")\n115\t        return res\n116\t"}]},"uuid":"93b3e0bd-63b4-4280-9f2d-e3eb1632d459","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:44.402Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9c9aac92-d72f-4505-85fb-96acf92a15fc","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"5a5d4fa9-0318-4683-aad1-1dedaa2cca48","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013Czf25THL3K7X5pmp8pTZJ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"KernelClassifier (Phase 7, P0.1) — the Python↔Rust WARDEN bridge. The authoritative danger\n2\tclassifier is Rust `kernel/src/tiers.rs::classify` (token-based, danger-first, fail-closed to A3),\n3\tcomposed with the raise-only registry pins in `Warden::decide`. Python's `Governor` otherwise\n4\tTRUSTS a Proposal's declared tier; this bridge lets the mesh DERIVE a tool's tier from that\n5\tfail-closed oracle instead of self-declaring it — so an Operator step or a Vision egress hop is\n6\ttiered by the same code that enforces at the kernel.\n7\t\n8\tConsulting the kernel is a pure A0 observation (`sigil-kernel classify` writes NO action-log\n9\trecord). Any failure — missing binary, timeout, non-zero exit, unparseable output, or an unknown\n10\ttier string — resolves to **A3** (fail-closed to the most-gated tier), mirroring the Rust\n11\tclassifier's own posture: never let a bridge error silently downgrade a dangerous tool.\"\"\"\n12\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n13\t\n14\timport json\n15\timport subprocess\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..voice.dispatch import _default_bin  # reuse the exact kernel-binary resolution\n18\tfrom .base import Tier\n19\t\n20\t_TIER = {\"A0\": Tier.A0, \"A1\": Tier.A1, \"A2\": Tier.A2, \"A3\": Tier.A3}\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tclass KernelClassifier:\n24\t    def __init__(self, kernel_bin: str | None = None, timeout: int = 15):\n25\t        self.kernel_bin = kernel_bin or _default_bin()\n26\t        self.timeout = timeout\n27\t\n28\t    def classify(self, tool: str) -> Tier:\n29\t        \"\"\"Return the WARDEN tier for `tool`. Fail-closed to A3 on ANY error/ambiguity.\"\"\"\n30\t        if not tool or not tool.strip():\n31\t            return Tier.A3\n32\t        try:\n33\t            proc = subprocess.run([self.kernel_bin, \"classify\", tool, \"--json\"],\n34\t                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n35\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n36\t            return Tier.A3\n37\t        if proc.returncode != 0:\n38\t            return Tier.A3\n39\t        try:\n40\t            data = json.loads(proc.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])\n41\t            return _TIER.get(str(data.get(\"tier\")), Tier.A3)\n42\t        except (ValueError, IndexError, KeyError, TypeError):\n43\t            return Tier.A3\n44\t"}]},"uuid":"ddd1b244-e60c-4132-8027-d07c69b84da5","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:44.404Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5a5d4fa9-0318-4683-aad1-1dedaa2cca48","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"85cf7a08-5ff8-452f-872b-f77b91878c9d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KK6HRo1FaH3rMJCA74BSzJ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Append-only, hash-chained JSONL spine (SIGIL §6.1, D1).\n2\t\n3\tReuses CRUCIBLE's tamper-evident chain verbatim (`sigil.reuse`): each line carries\n4\t`{seq, prev_hash, entry_hash}` where `entry_hash` links prev+cert_digest+seq. The\n5\t`cert_digest` is over the record's CONTENT only (scope/kind/source/actor/payload/\n6\tparent/supersedes) — NOT the wallclock `ts` — so the chain is replay-stable. Appends\n7\tare O(1): read the last line's entry, `append_entry`, write.\n8\t\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\timport json\n12\timport os\n13\tfrom pathlib import Path\n14\tfrom typing import Any, Iterator\n15\t\n16\tfrom ..config import SCOPE, SPINE_PATH\n17\tfrom ..reuse import ChainEntry, append_entry, build_chain, digest_payload, verify_chain\n18\tfrom .models import SpineRecord, now_iso\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef _last_nonempty_line(path: Path) -> str | None:\n22\t    \"\"\"Read the last non-empty line without loading the file (seek-from-end).\"\"\"\n23\t    with path.open(\"rb\") as f:\n24\t        f.seek(0, os.SEEK_END)\n25\t        end = f.tell()\n26\t        if end == 0:\n27\t            return None\n28\t        buf = b\"\"\n29\t        pos = end\n30\t        while pos > 0:\n31\t            step = min(8192, pos)\n32\t            pos -= step\n33\t            f.seek(pos)\n34\t            buf = f.read(step) + buf\n35\t            lines = [ln for ln in buf.split(b\"\\n\") if ln.strip()]\n36\t            if lines and (pos == 0 or buf.count(b\"\\n\") >= 2):\n37\t                return lines[-1].decode(\"utf-8\")\n38\t        lines = [ln for ln in buf.split(b\"\\n\") if ln.strip()]\n39\t        return lines[-1].decode(\"utf-8\") if lines else None\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tclass SpineStore:\n43\t    def __init__(self, path: Path | str = SPINE_PATH) -> None:\n44\t        self.path = Path(path)\n45\t        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n46\t        self._last: ChainEntry | None = self._read_last_entry()\n47\t\n48\t    # --- write --------------------------------------------------------------------\n49\t    def append(\n50\t        self, *, kind: str, source: str, actor: str, payload: dict[str, Any],\n51\t        parent_id: int | None = None, supersedes_id: int | None = None,\n52\t        ts: str | None = None,\n53\t    ) -> int:\n54\t        content = {\n55\t            \"scope\": SCOPE, \"kind\": kind, \"source\": source, \"actor\": actor,\n56\t            \"payload\": payload, \"parent_id\": parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": supersedes_id,\n57\t        }\n58\t        cert_digest = digest_payload(content)  # wallclock-free\n59\t        entry = append_entry([self._last], cert_digest) if self._last else build_chain([cert_digest])[0]\n60\t        record = {\n61\t            \"seq\": entry.seq, **content, \"ts\": ts or now_iso(),\n62\t            \"cert_digest\": cert_digest, \"prev_hash\": entry.prev_hash, \"entry_hash\": entry.entry_hash,\n63\t        }\n64\t        with self.path.open(\"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n65\t            f.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n66\t        self._last = entry\n67\t        return entry.seq\n68\t\n69\t    # --- read ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n70\t    def iter_records(self, *, since_seq: int = -1) -> Iterator[SpineRecord]:\n71\t        if not self.path.exists():\n72\t            return\n73\t        with self.path.open(encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n74\t            for line in f:\n75\t                line = line.strip()\n76\t                if not line:\n77\t                    continue\n78\t                d = json.loads(line)\n79\t                if d[\"seq\"] > since_seq:\n80\t                    yield SpineRecord.from_dict(d)\n81\t\n82\t    def get(self, seq: int) -> SpineRecord | None:\n83\t        for r in self.iter_records(since_seq=seq - 1):\n84\t            if r.seq == seq:\n85\t                return r\n86\t            if r.seq > seq:\n87\t                break\n88\t        return None\n89\t\n90\t    @property\n91\t    def next_seq(self) -> int:\n92\t        return (self._last.seq + 1) if self._last else 0\n93\t\n94\t    def count(self) -> int:\n95\t        return sum(1 for _ in self.iter_records())\n96\t\n97\t    # --- integrity ----------------------------------------------------------------\n98\t    def entries(self) -> list[ChainEntry]:\n99\t        return [\n100\t            ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash)\n101\t            for r in self.iter_records()\n102\t        ]\n103\t\n104\t    def verify(self) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n105\t        \"\"\"Two-layer UNKEYED integrity: (1) BINDING — each record's payload still hashes to its\n106\t        stored cert_digest (catches silent payload edits); (2) CHAIN — the entries link cleanly\n107\t        (catches delete/reorder/entry tamper). This proves internal CONSISTENCY, not authenticity:\n108\t        a naive payload edit fails (1), and a mid-chain digest edit cascades an entry_hash/prev_hash\n109\t        break caught by (2) — BUT a writer who recomputes cert_digest+entry_hash for the tip (no\n110\t        successor to cascade into) or forward-cascades a fork produces a self-consistent chain that\n111\t        passes here. Resistance to a recompute-capable writer is the owner-SIGNED head's job\n112\t        (`checkpoint.verify_checkpoint`, Ed25519 + monotonic last_seq). Use this for corruption/\n113\t        naive-tamper detection; use the signed head for tamper-EVIDENCE.\"\"\"\n114\t        entries: list[ChainEntry] = []\n115\t        for r in self.iter_records():\n116\t            content = {\n117\t                \"scope\": r.scope, \"kind\": r.kind, \"source\": r.source, \"actor\": r.actor,\n118\t                \"payload\": r.payload, \"parent_id\": r.parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": r.supersedes_id,\n119\t            }\n120\t            if digest_payload(content) != r.cert_digest:\n121\t                return False, f\"binding break at seq {r.seq}: payload does not match cert_digest (record tampered)\"\n122\t            entries.append(ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash))\n123\t        return verify_chain(entries)\n124\t\n125\t    def _read_last_entry(self) -> ChainEntry | None:\n126\t        line = _last_nonempty_line(self.path) if self.path.exists() else None\n127\t        if not line:\n128\t            return None\n129\t        d = json.loads(line)\n130\t        return ChainEntry(seq=d[\"seq\"], prev_hash=d[\"prev_hash\"], cert_digest=d[\"cert_digest\"], entry_hash=d[\"entry_hash\"])\n131\t"}]},"uuid":"477b4b1b-7318-4f5c-9538-8d11e1bf5653","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:54.164Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"85cf7a08-5ff8-452f-872b-f77b91878c9d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"477b4b1b-7318-4f5c-9538-8d11e1bf5653","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAA59CzRZpuua8yC3DBB","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01BpNeyLpzXDCcu8dQkc6Cyb","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/governor/budget.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":7618,"cache_read_input_tokens":13604,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":7618,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAA581JjzYWm7RjXfUg8","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"5b6a9f85-e3b9-4662-9f7d-59abd74626c4","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:54.661Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"5b6a9f85-e3b9-4662-9f7d-59abd74626c4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BpNeyLpzXDCcu8dQkc6Cyb","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Per-agent daily budgets (SIGIL §5) — action and interrupt caps, enforced FAIL-CLOSED (at cap →\n2\tdeny). The spine IS the ledger: spend is derived by counting an agent's own records for the UTC day,\n3\tso there is no separate counter to drift or forge. Caps default to None (uncapped) → budgets are\n4\topt-in and the default dispatch behavior is unchanged; a configured cap is hard-enforced.\n5\t\n6\tToken/cost caps are a documented seam: the mesh does not yet meter provider tokens per action, so\n7\t`daily_actions`/`daily_interrupts` are the enforced dimensions today (interrupt = an A1 event/alert —\n8\tthe SENTINEL failure mode §4.3).\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n12\tfrom typing import Optional, Tuple\n13\t\n14\t\n15\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n16\tclass BudgetCaps:\n17\t    daily_actions: Optional[int] = None       # max records an agent may write per UTC day\n18\t    daily_interrupts: Optional[int] = None    # max A1 events/alerts (the noise budget) per UTC day\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tclass BudgetLedger:\n22\t    def __init__(self, store, caps: Optional[BudgetCaps] = None):\n23\t        self.store = store\n24\t        self.caps = caps or BudgetCaps()\n25\t\n26\t    def spent(self, agent: str, day_iso: str) -> Tuple[int, int]:\n27\t        \"\"\"(#records this agent wrote today, #of those that were interrupt events). `day_iso` is the\n28\t        UTC date prefix (YYYY-MM-DD) — the ts is informational, so bucketing is by wallclock date.\"\"\"\n29\t        actions = interrupts = 0\n30\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n31\t            if r.source != \"agent\" or r.actor != agent:\n32\t                continue\n33\t            if not (r.ts or \"\").startswith(day_iso):\n34\t                continue\n35\t            # count only actions actually TAKEN (auto/queued) — a denial consumes no budget, so a\n36\t            # flood of denied attempts can't self-reinforce the cap.\n37\t            if r.payload.get(\"decision\") not in (\"auto\", \"queued\"):\n38\t                continue\n39\t            actions += 1\n40\t            if r.kind == \"event\":\n41\t                interrupts += 1\n42\t        return actions, interrupts\n43\t\n44\t    def over_budget(self, agent: str, day_iso: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:\n45\t        if self.caps.daily_actions is None and self.caps.daily_interrupts is None:\n46\t            return False, \"\"\n47\t        actions, interrupts = self.spent(agent, day_iso)\n48\t        if self.caps.daily_actions is not None and actions >= self.caps.daily_actions:\n49\t            return True, f\"daily action cap ({self.caps.daily_actions}) reached for {agent}\"\n50\t        if self.caps.daily_interrupts is not None and interrupts >= self.caps.daily_interrupts:\n51\t            return True, f\"daily interrupt cap ({self.caps.daily_interrupts}) reached for {agent}\"\n52\t        return False, \"\"\n53\t"}]},"uuid":"f5daa3e4-3258-4476-a6dc-f27f238bdead","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:54.672Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5b6a9f85-e3b9-4662-9f7d-59abd74626c4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"f5daa3e4-3258-4476-a6dc-f27f238bdead","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAA59CzRZpuua8yC3DBB","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01XS4RkvCpPsgNRg2dd7YMQ1","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/consolidate/gate.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":7618,"cache_read_input_tokens":13604,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":7618,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAA581JjzYWm7RjXfUg8","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"bd03c853-9440-47c4-b335-23e347dd6694","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:55.307Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"bd03c853-9440-47c4-b335-23e347dd6694","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XS4RkvCpPsgNRg2dd7YMQ1","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The veracity gate — SIGIL's demote-only admission choke point (ported from\n2\tveracity/firewall.py's re-execute-don't-string-trust discipline).\n3\t\n4\tThe gate grounds the QUOTE, and the quote — the verbatim record span — is what becomes the\n5\tserved fact. The model's free-text `statement` is NEVER the authoritative content (a\n6\tred-team re-check proved a token-subset check over the statement is not entailment: an\n7\textractor can drop a negation or reorder words to invert meaning while staying a token\n8\tsubset). By certifying and serving the verbatim quote instead, a fabricated or inverted\n9\tstatement can never be presented as a grounded fact — the owner only ever sees their own words.\n10\t\n11\tA candidate GROUNDS iff:\n12\t  1. it cites ≥1 seq, every cited seq is inside the exact window fed to the extractor;\n13\t  2. its `quote` is verbatim in ≥1 cited record re-fetched from the spine (never the model's copy);\n14\t  3. the quote is SPECIFIC — it carries ≥2 salient (non-stopword) tokens, Unicode-aware, so a\n15\t     trivial common substring grounds nothing.\n16\t\n17\tThe gate can only ever DEMOTE; the model's confidence never enters. Anything that fails is\n18\trecorded honestly as commentary, never as a fact and never dropped.\"\"\"\n19\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n20\t\n21\timport re\n22\t\n23\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n24\tfrom .grounding import UNGROUNDED, ground_tag\n25\tfrom .models import CandidateFact, GateVerdict\n26\t\n27\t_WS = re.compile(r\"\\s+\")\n28\t# Unicode-aware: a token starts with a word char (letter/digit, any script) and may carry\n29\t# internal separators (dates 2026-07-20, versions v1.2, paths a/b). \\w is Unicode by default.\n30\t_TOKEN = re.compile(r\"[^\\W_][\\w'./-]*\", re.UNICODE)\n31\t\n32\t_STOPWORDS = frozenset({\n33\t    \"the\", \"and\", \"for\", \"with\", \"that\", \"this\", \"have\", \"has\", \"had\", \"was\", \"were\", \"are\",\n34\t    \"will\", \"would\", \"should\", \"could\", \"can\", \"but\", \"you\", \"your\", \"our\", \"their\",\n35\t    \"from\", \"into\", \"out\", \"about\", \"then\", \"than\", \"them\", \"they\", \"there\", \"here\",\n36\t    \"what\", \"when\", \"where\", \"which\", \"who\", \"how\", \"all\", \"any\", \"some\", \"get\", \"got\", \"let\",\n37\t    \"let's\", \"lets\", \"actually\", \"just\", \"now\", \"today\", \"yesterday\", \"tomorrow\", \"really\",\n38\t    \"very\", \"more\", \"most\", \"much\", \"many\", \"also\", \"like\", \"want\", \"need\", \"going\", \"gonna\",\n39\t})\n40\t\n41\tMIN_QUOTE_SALIENT = 2      # a quote below this is too trivial to ground a claim\n42\t\n43\t\n44\tdef _norm(text: str) -> str:\n45\t    return _WS.sub(\" \", (text or \"\")).strip().lower()\n46\t\n47\t\n48\tdef salient(text: str) -> set[str]:\n49\t    \"\"\"Unicode-aware salient tokens: ≥3 chars, not a stopword, punctuation-trimmed.\"\"\"\n50\t    out: set[str] = set()\n51\t    for t in _TOKEN.findall((text or \"\").casefold()):\n52\t        t = t.strip(\"._/-'\")\n53\t        if len(t) >= 3 and t not in _STOPWORDS:\n54\t            out.add(t)\n55\t    return out\n56\t\n57\t\n58\tdef _real_span(quote: str, record_text: str) -> str | None:\n59\t    \"\"\"Return the BYTE-verbatim span of `record_text` that `quote` matches (tolerant of\n60\t    whitespace/case differences in the model's copy), so the SERVED fact is the record's own\n61\t    bytes, not the model's transcription. None if not found.\"\"\"\n62\t    words = quote.split()\n63\t    if not words:\n64\t        return None\n65\t    pat = r\"\\s+\".join(re.escape(w) for w in words)\n66\t    m = re.search(pat, record_text, re.IGNORECASE)\n67\t    return m.group(0) if m else None\n68\t\n69\t\n70\tdef admit(cand: CandidateFact, window_seqs: set[int], store: SpineStore) -> GateVerdict:\n71\t    \"\"\"Re-execute the candidate's citation(s) and ground the VERBATIM QUOTE(s). A contradiction\n72\t    must verify ≥2 DISTINCT records (evidence from both sides); every other kind needs ≥1. The\n73\t    served `text` is the record's own byte-verbatim span(s). Any failure → UNGROUNDED.\"\"\"\n74\t    if not cand.source_seqs:\n75\t        return GateVerdict(False, UNGROUNDED, [], \"cites no source records — cannot ground\")\n76\t\n77\t    outside = sorted({s for s in cand.source_seqs if s not in window_seqs})\n78\t    if outside:\n79\t        return GateVerdict(False, UNGROUNDED, [],\n80\t                           f\"cites seq(s) outside the fed window {outside} — fabricated citation\")\n81\t\n82\t    quotes = [q for q in (cand.quotes or (cand.quote,)) if q and q.strip()]\n83\t    if not quotes:\n84\t        return GateVerdict(False, UNGROUNDED, [], \"no quote to verify\")\n85\t\n86\t    verified: set[int] = set()\n87\t    spans: list[str] = []\n88\t    for qq in quotes:\n89\t        if len(salient(qq)) < MIN_QUOTE_SALIENT:\n90\t            continue                                          # a trivial quote grounds nothing\n91\t        qn = _norm(qq)\n92\t        for s in cand.source_seqs:\n93\t            rec = store.get(s)\n94\t            if rec is not None and s not in verified and qn in _norm(rec.text()):\n95\t                verified.add(s)\n96\t                spans.append(_real_span(qq, rec.text()) or qq)\n97\t                break\n98\t    if not verified:\n99\t        return GateVerdict(False, UNGROUNDED, [],\n100\t                           \"no quote is verbatim-and-specific in any cited record — fabricated/trivial quote\")\n101\t\n102\t    need = 2 if cand.kind == \"contradiction\" else 1\n103\t    if len(verified) < need:\n104\t        return GateVerdict(False, UNGROUNDED, [],\n105\t                           f\"a {cand.kind} needs ≥{need} distinct records to verbatim-verify — only {len(verified)} did\")\n106\t\n107\t    vs = sorted(verified)\n108\t    return GateVerdict(True, ground_tag(min(vs)), vs,\n109\t                       f\"grounded: {len(vs)} verbatim record span(s) at seq(s) {vs}\",\n110\t                       text=\" || \".join(spans))\n111\t"}]},"uuid":"48b652ee-6177-4a5b-ac97-8fa0063975d3","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:55.347Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"bd03c853-9440-47c4-b335-23e347dd6694","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"413228a4-1fc5-4740-aa33-d143f32fc164","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015Yo1LLmSscX3p4XYbN9hYy","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//! WARDEN autonomy tiers (SIGIL §5). Every tool invocation is classified A0–A3; the gate\n2\t//! decision follows from the tier. FAIL-CLOSED and TOKEN-BASED: the tool name is split into\n3\t//! whole tokens (on `.`/`_`/`-`/`/`/space) and matched against whole-token sets — never a raw\n4\t//! substring, so \"overwrite\" is not \"write\" and \"forget\" is not \"get\". Danger is checked FIRST,\n5\t//! and A0 (auto, un-queued) is reachable ONLY via a positive safe-verb allowlist; anything not\n6\t//! positively classified — including any unknown or dangerous-target tool — is A3.\n7\t\n8\tuse std::fmt;\n9\t\n10\tuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n11\t\n12\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n13\tpub enum Tier {\n14\t    /// Observe / answer — memory queries, screen read, voice reply. Auto.\n15\t    A0,\n16\t    /// Reversible internal act — write a report/brief, draft-branch, raise an alert. Auto + logged.\n17\t    A1,\n18\t    /// External-visible / semi-reversible — send email, calendar write, purchase < cap. Queued.\n19\t    A2,\n20\t    /// Destructive / financial / security — push protected branch, deploy, delete, spend > cap. Explicit.\n21\t    A3,\n22\t}\n23\t\n24\timpl Tier {\n25\t    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {\n26\t        match self {\n27\t            Tier::A0 => \"A0\",\n28\t            Tier::A1 => \"A1\",\n29\t            Tier::A2 => \"A2\",\n30\t            Tier::A3 => \"A3\",\n31\t        }\n32\t    }\n33\t}\n34\t\n35\timpl fmt::Display for Tier {\n36\t    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n37\t        f.write_str(self.as_str())\n38\t    }\n39\t}\n40\t\n41\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n42\tpub enum Decision {\n43\t    Auto,\n44\t    Queued,\n45\t    ExplicitRequired,\n46\t}\n47\t\n48\timpl Decision {\n49\t    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {\n50\t        match self {\n51\t            Decision::Auto => \"auto\",\n52\t            Decision::Queued => \"queued\",\n53\t            Decision::ExplicitRequired => \"explicit-required\",\n54\t        }\n55\t    }\n56\t    pub fn may_run(&self) -> bool {\n57\t        matches!(self, Decision::Auto)\n58\t    }\n59\t}\n60\t\n61\t/// A3 — destructive verbs, financial ops, crypto/restore ops, and DANGEROUS TARGETS\n62\t/// (secret/identity/network/prod material is A3 regardless of the verb: `secrets.read`,\n63\t/// `iam.policy.write`, `budget.get`, `vault.read`).\n64\tconst A3_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n65\t    // destructive verbs\n66\t    \"push\", \"deploy\", \"delete\", \"destroy\", \"drop\", \"remove\", \"purge\", \"wipe\", \"truncate\",\n67\t    \"overwrite\", \"erase\", \"format\", \"kill\", \"shutdown\", \"reboot\", \"reset\", \"disable\", \"enable\",\n68\t    \"override\", \"force\", \"sudo\", \"exec\", \"eval\", \"chmod\", \"chown\", \"patch\", \"install\", \"uninstall\",\n69\t    // crypto + restore/rollback (a restore silently reverts current state)\n70\t    \"encrypt\", \"decrypt\", \"restore\", \"revert\", \"rollback\", \"recover\",\n71\t    // financial\n72\t    \"spend\", \"purchase\", \"pay\", \"payment\", \"transaction\", \"transfer\", \"refund\", \"invoice\",\n73\t    \"allocate\", \"budget\", \"release\", \"sign\",\n74\t    // security / identity / infra / secret-store TARGETS (dangerous no matter the verb)\n75\t    \"secret\", \"secrets\", \"credential\", \"credentials\", \"token\", \"tokens\", \"key\", \"keys\", \"iam\",\n76\t    \"policy\", \"firewall\", \"acl\", \"role\", \"grant\", \"revoke\", \"rotate\", \"escalate\", \"infra\",\n77\t    \"prod\", \"production\", \"env\", \"master\", \"root\", \"admin\",\n78\t    \"vault\", \"keyring\", \"keychain\", \"keystore\", \"hsm\",\n79\t];\n80\t\n81\t/// A2 — external-visible / semi-reversible: communication, publishing, and bulk DATA EGRESS\n82\t/// (export/dump of a private store must be human-seen, never auto).\n83\tconst A2_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n84\t    \"send\", \"email\", \"smtp\", \"publish\", \"post\", \"message\", \"outbound\", \"webhook\", \"sms\", \"notify\",\n85\t    \"share\", \"upload\", \"invite\", \"calendar\", \"tweet\", \"dm\", \"export\", \"dump\", \"download\", \"sync\",\n86\t];\n87\t\n88\t/// A1 — reversible internal writes (drafts, notes, memory writes, non-push commits). NOTE:\n89\t/// \"snapshot\" is intentionally NOT here — a snapshot RESTORE is destructive (caught by A3\n90\t/// \"restore\"), and snapshot-create alone falls to fail-closed A3 rather than risk auto-run.\n91\tconst A1_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n92\t    \"write\", \"note\", \"brief\", \"report\", \"draft\", \"alert\", \"consolidate\", \"commit\", \"branch\",\n93\t    \"annotate\", \"tag\", \"label\",\n94\t];\n95\t\n96\t/// A0 — POSITIVE allowlist of known-safe observe/answer VERBS only (NO target nouns — a target\n97\t/// noun here would let e.g. `memory.encrypt` auto-run). A tool reaches A0 only by a read-verb\n98\t/// here, or an exact name in `A0_TOOLS`; absence of danger alone is NOT sufficient → fail-closed A3.\n99\tconst A0_VERBS: &[&str] = &[\n100\t    \"read\", \"search\", \"query\", \"get\", \"list\", \"status\", \"recall\", \"observe\", \"answer\",\n101\t    \"view\", \"show\", \"find\", \"frame\", \"peek\", \"describe\", \"inspect\", \"lookup\",\n102\t];\n103\t\n104\t/// A0 — exact-name allowlist for the known read-only memory tools whose names are not verb-clean\n105\t/// (`graph.entity`, `episodic.range`, `threads.open`, `commitments.due`, `contradictions.pending`,\n106\t/// `ingest.status`). Matched on the whole lowercased tool name.\n107\tconst A0_TOOLS: &[&str] = &[\n108\t    \"memory.search\", \"graph.query\", \"graph.entity\", \"episodic.range\", \"ingest.status\",\n109\t    \"threads.open\", \"commitments.due\", \"contradictions.pending\",\n110\t];\n111\t\n112\t/// Split a tool name into whole tokens on `.`/`_`/`-`/`/` and whitespace, lowercased.\n113\tfn tokens(tool: &str) -> Vec<String> {\n114\t    tool.split(|c: char| c == '.' || c == '_' || c == '-' || c == '/' || c.is_whitespace())\n115\t        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())\n116\t        .map(|s| s.to_ascii_lowercase())\n117\t        .collect()\n118\t}\n119\t\n120\t/// Classify a tool name into a tier. Danger FIRST, then A2, A1; A0 only via a positive safe-verb\n121\t/// or an exact safe-tool name; everything else (unknown / not-positively-safe / empty) → A3.\n122\tpub fn classify(tool: &str) -> Tier {\n123\t    let full = tool.to_ascii_lowercase();\n124\t    let tk = tokens(tool);\n125\t    if tk.is_empty() {\n126\t        return Tier::A3;\n127\t    }\n128\t    let has = |set: &[&str]| tk.iter().any(|t| set.contains(&t.as_str()));\n129\t    if has(A3_TOKENS) {\n130\t        return Tier::A3;\n131\t    }\n132\t    if has(A2_TOKENS) {\n133\t        return Tier::A2;\n134\t    }\n135\t    if has(A1_TOKENS) {\n136\t        return Tier::A1;\n137\t    }\n138\t    if A0_TOOLS.contains(&full.as_str()) || has(A0_VERBS) {\n139\t        return Tier::A0;\n140\t    }\n141\t    Tier::A3 // not positively classified → fail-closed to the most-gated tier\n142\t}\n143\t\n144\tpub fn gate(tier: Tier) -> Decision {\n145\t    match tier {\n146\t        Tier::A0 | Tier::A1 => Decision::Auto,\n147\t        Tier::A2 => Decision::Queued,\n148\t        Tier::A3 => Decision::ExplicitRequired,\n149\t    }\n150\t}\n151\t\n152\t#[cfg(test)]\n153\tmod tests {\n154\t    use super::*;\n155\t\n156\t    #[test]\n157\t    fn classification_matches_spec_examples() {\n158\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.search\"), Tier::A0);\n159\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"graph.query\"), Tier::A0);\n160\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.write\"), Tier::A1);\n161\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"brief.compose\"), Tier::A1);\n162\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"email.send\"), Tier::A2);\n163\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"calendar.write\"), Tier::A2); // \"calendar\" (A2) beats \"write\" (A1)\n164\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"git.push\"), Tier::A3);\n165\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"deploy.prod\"), Tier::A3);\n166\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"data.delete\"), Tier::A3);\n167\t    }\n168\t\n169\t    #[test]\n170\t    fn unknown_and_empty_are_fail_closed_a3() {\n171\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"something.unclassified\"), Tier::A3);\n172\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"\"), Tier::A3);\n173\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"weird\"), Tier::A3);\n174\t        assert_eq!(gate(classify(\"something.unclassified\")), Decision::ExplicitRequired);\n175\t    }\n176\t\n177\t    #[test]\n178\t    fn substring_collisions_do_not_lower_the_tier() {\n179\t        // the exact review findings: a dangerous op must NOT ride a benign substring down a tier\n180\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"config.overwrite\"), Tier::A3, \"overwrite != write\");\n181\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"db.overwrite\"), Tier::A3);\n182\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"files.forget\"), Tier::A3, \"forget != get; fail-closed\");\n183\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"budget.override\"), Tier::A3, \"financial + override\");\n184\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"budget.get\"), Tier::A3, \"financial target is A3 regardless of verb\");\n185\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"iam.policy.write\"), Tier::A3, \"identity write is A3, not A1\");\n186\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"firewall.rule.write\"), Tier::A3);\n187\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"transaction.sign\"), Tier::A3, \"financial signing is A3, not A1\");\n188\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"secrets.read\"), Tier::A3, \"secret read is A3, not A0\");\n189\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"credentials.get\"), Tier::A3);\n190\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"account.disable\"), Tier::A3);\n191\t    }\n192\t\n193\t    #[test]\n194\t    fn dangerous_ops_on_safe_targets_do_not_auto_run() {\n195\t        // the CRITICAL re-check finding: a target noun must NOT grant A0 to a dangerous verb\n196\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.encrypt\"), Tier::A3, \"ransomware-shaped op must NOT be A0\");\n197\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"graph.destroy\"), Tier::A3);\n198\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"entity.merge\"), Tier::A3, \"irreversible rewrite → fail-closed, not A0\");\n199\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.export\"), Tier::A2, \"full-store egress must be queued, not auto\");\n200\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.dump\"), Tier::A2);\n201\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"snapshot.restore\"), Tier::A3, \"a restore reverts state → A3\");\n202\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"vault.read\"), Tier::A3, \"secret-store read is A3 regardless of verb\");\n203\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"keyring.get\"), Tier::A3);\n204\t    }\n205\t\n206\t    #[test]\n207\t    fn safe_memory_tools_still_reach_a0() {\n208\t        for t in [\n209\t            \"memory.search\", \"graph.query\", \"graph.entity\", \"episodic.range\", \"ingest.status\",\n210\t            \"threads.open\", \"commitments.due\", \"contradictions.pending\",\n211\t        ] {\n212\t            assert_eq!(classify(t), Tier::A0, \"{t} is a read-only memory tool → A0\");\n213\t        }\n214\t    }\n215\t\n216\t    #[test]\n217\t    fn danger_token_wins_over_safe_token_regardless_of_order() {\n218\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"read.then.delete\"), Tier::A3);\n219\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"delete.and.list\"), Tier::A3);\n220\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"search.and.deploy\"), Tier::A3);\n221\t    }\n222\t\n223\t    #[test]\n224\t    fn gates_are_correct() {\n225\t        assert!(gate(Tier::A0).may_run());\n226\t        assert!(gate(Tier::A1).may_run());\n227\t        assert!(!gate(Tier::A2).may_run());\n228\t        assert!(!gate(Tier::A3).may_run());\n229\t    }\n230\t}\n231\t"}]},"uuid":"738ad168-bcd6-4f93-a661-ae0bed674108","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:55.569Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"413228a4-1fc5-4740-aa33-d143f32fc164","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"db075f9c-d702-4216-941e-50b6286c8f08","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VWSTcQ1iC6mQnPXuXhFNzB","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The episodic spine record — one immutable event, source of truth (SIGIL §6.1).\"\"\"\n2\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n3\t\n4\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n5\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n6\tfrom typing import Any\n7\t\n8\t# event kinds (superset of the CRUCIBLE blackboard EventKind, extended for SIGIL sources)\n9\tKINDS = frozenset({\n10\t    \"message\", \"tool_call\", \"tool_result\", \"decision\", \"commitment\",\n11\t    \"commit\", \"document\", \"brief\", \"refusal\", \"email\", \"session\",\n12\t    \"entity\", \"contradiction\",  # promoted by the ARCHIVIST consolidation pass (§6.3)\n13\t    \"warden_checkpoint\",        # WARDEN (Phase 1) cross-anchors its action-log head here (anti-rollback)\n14\t    \"event\", \"finding\", \"interaction\", \"draft\",  # the agent mesh (Phase 3, §4): SENTINEL/BASTION/ENVOY\n15\t    \"report\", \"pr\",             # Phase 4: SCHOLAR sourced research reports, ARTIFICER PR proposals\n16\t})\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tdef now_iso() -> str:\n20\t    \"\"\"Informational wallclock timestamp. NOT part of the content digest (replay-stable).\"\"\"\n21\t    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n22\t\n23\t\n24\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n25\tclass SpineRecord:\n26\t    \"\"\"One line of the spine. `cert_digest`/`prev_hash`/`entry_hash` are the chain fields;\n27\t    everything above `ts` is the digested content (ts is informational, excluded from the digest).\"\"\"\n28\t    seq: int\n29\t    scope: str\n30\t    kind: str\n31\t    source: str            # \"claude-code\" | \"git\" | \"doc\" | ...\n32\t    actor: str             # \"user\" | \"assistant\" | session id | commit author | ...\n33\t    payload: dict[str, Any]\n34\t    parent_id: int | None\n35\t    supersedes_id: int | None\n36\t    ts: str\n37\t    cert_digest: str\n38\t    prev_hash: str\n39\t    entry_hash: str\n40\t\n41\t    @classmethod\n42\t    def from_dict(cls, d: dict[str, Any]) -> \"SpineRecord\":\n43\t        return cls(\n44\t            seq=d[\"seq\"], scope=d[\"scope\"], kind=d[\"kind\"], source=d[\"source\"],\n45\t            actor=d[\"actor\"], payload=d.get(\"payload\") or {},\n46\t            parent_id=d.get(\"parent_id\"), supersedes_id=d.get(\"supersedes_id\"),\n47\t            ts=d.get(\"ts\", \"\"), cert_digest=d[\"cert_digest\"],\n48\t            prev_hash=d[\"prev_hash\"], entry_hash=d[\"entry_hash\"],\n49\t        )\n50\t\n51\t    def text(self) -> str:\n52\t        \"\"\"Best-effort human/searchable text of the record (for embedding + display).\"\"\"\n53\t        p = self.payload or {}\n54\t        for key in (\"text\", \"content\", \"message\", \"summary\", \"body\", \"title\"):\n55\t            v = p.get(key)\n56\t            if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip():\n57\t                return v\n58\t        # tool records / structured payloads: compact JSON fallback\n59\t        import json\n60\t        return json.dumps(p, ensure_ascii=False)[:4000]\n61\t"}]},"uuid":"8edc3dab-087f-4fe3-a55d-883f5020593e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:31:05.378Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"db075f9c-d702-4216-941e-50b6286c8f08","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"6915eba6-5ef2-41a9-ac43-57fd625ae035","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01U9jHza1FnVsMDNFQrACY6d","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The SIGIL agent mesh framework (SIGIL §4/§5). Each agent has a MANDATE and an AUTONOMY\n2\tCEILING (A0–A3); everything it does is a `Proposal` carrying a tier. Proposals at or below the\n3\tauto bar (A1) and at or below the agent's ceiling are AUTO-APPLIED (written to the spine);\n4\tanything higher is QUEUED as a pending record a human must approve — never auto-executed.\n5\t\n6\tThe doctrine is enforced STRUCTURALLY, not by discipline: an agent literally cannot take an\n7\texternal-effect action, because no agent has a send/deploy/spend code path. ENVOY, in\n8\tparticular, drafts only — its ceiling is A2 and there is no method that transmits. Agent output\n9\tis provenance-linked spine records, so a brief or a draft is auditable back to what produced it.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n13\tfrom enum import IntEnum\n14\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n15\t\n16\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n17\t\n18\tAGENTS_SOURCE = \"agent\"\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tclass Tier(IntEnum):\n22\t    \"\"\"Mirror of the WARDEN tiers (SIGIL §5), ordered so `<=` compares autonomy.\"\"\"\n23\t    A0 = 0   # observe / answer\n24\t    A1 = 1   # reversible internal act (write a report/brief/event) — the AUTO bar\n25\t    A2 = 2   # external-visible / semi-reversible (send, calendar write) — QUEUED\n26\t    A3 = 3   # destructive / financial / security — explicit, never auto\n27\t\n28\t    def label(self) -> str:\n29\t        return self.name\n30\t\n31\t\n32\tAUTO_BAR = Tier.A1  # A0/A1 auto-apply; A2/A3 queue for human approval\n33\t\n34\t\n35\t@dataclass\n36\tclass Proposal:\n37\t    \"\"\"One thing an agent wants to do: emit a record (auto) or take a gated action (queued).\"\"\"\n38\t    kind: str                       # spine record kind (event/finding/brief/interaction/draft/...)\n39\t    payload: dict\n40\t    tier: Tier = Tier.A1\n41\t    parent_id: Optional[int] = None\n42\t    supersedes_id: Optional[int] = None   # this record supersedes an earlier one (e.g. a resolution)\n43\t\n44\t\n45\t@dataclass\n46\tclass AgentResult:\n47\t    agent: str\n48\t    applied: List[int] = field(default_factory=list)     # seqs written (auto)\n49\t    queued: List[dict] = field(default_factory=list)      # proposals held for approval\n50\t    notes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tclass Agent:\n54\t    \"\"\"Base agent. Subclasses implement `run()`; the base routes every Proposal through the GOVERNOR\n55\t    (SIGIL §5: kill switch, budgets, promotion — Phase 6) and records the outcome on the spine.\n56\t    Backward compatible: with no kill/cap/promotion the governor reproduces the original gate.\"\"\"\n57\t    name: str = \"AGENT\"\n58\t    mandate: str = \"\"\n59\t    ceiling: Tier = Tier.A1\n60\t\n61\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, governor=None):\n62\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n63\t        if governor is None:\n64\t            from ..governor import Governor\n65\t            governor = Governor(self.store)\n66\t        self.governor = governor\n67\t\n68\t    def run(self) -> AgentResult:\n69\t        raise NotImplementedError\n70\t\n71\t    def _dispatch(self, proposals: List[Proposal]) -> AgentResult:\n72\t        from ..governor import Outcome\n73\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n74\t        for p in proposals:\n75\t            effective = max(p.tier, Tier.A0)\n76\t            # promotion scope = the RECORD KIND (the real action written), not a self-asserted label:\n77\t            # a promotion is \"this agent may auto-approve A2 records of this kind\" (red-pen RP-3).\n78\t            decision = self.governor.decide(agent=self.name, tier=effective,\n79\t                                            ceiling=self.ceiling, scope=p.kind)\n80\t            common = {**p.payload, \"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n81\t                      \"governor\": decision.reason}\n82\t            if decision.outcome == Outcome.AUTO:\n83\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n84\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"auto\"},\n85\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n86\t                res.applied.append(seq)\n87\t            elif decision.outcome == Outcome.QUEUE:\n88\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n89\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"queued\", \"status\": \"awaiting-approval\"},\n90\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n91\t                res.queued.append({\"seq\": seq, \"kind\": p.kind, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n92\t                                   \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\") or p.payload.get(\"to\")})\n93\t            else:  # DENY — kill switch or budget; record the refusal, take no action\n94\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n95\t                                  payload={\"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n96\t                                           \"decision\": \"denied\", \"governor\": decision.reason,\n97\t                                           \"of_kind\": p.kind, \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\")},\n98\t                                  parent_id=p.parent_id)\n99\t                res.notes.append(f\"DENIED {p.kind} ({effective.label()}): {decision.reason}\")\n100\t        return res\n101\t"}]},"uuid":"05572e76-6810-43a7-b840-a439fa8bf489","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:31:05.715Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6915eba6-5ef2-41a9-ac43-57fd625ae035","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
