{"parentUuid":"6094a080-c62b-435d-a1c3-aa6675b487db","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Ljwg6Da4Sqm8nLtx71txGA","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SIGIL governor (Phase 6, SIGIL §5 mechanics) — the runtime enforcement of the WARDEN autonomy\n2\tmodel for the AGENT MESH (the Rust `kernel/` WARDEN governs kernel-level actions; this is its\n3\tPython-side mirror for agents). Every agent Proposal is decided here BEFORE it is written:\n4\t\n5\t  • KILL SWITCH (sealed latch): when engaged, the mesh is halted — only A0 observe/read survives.\n6\t  • BUDGETS: per-agent daily action/interrupt caps, fail-closed (over budget → deny + log).\n7\t  • PROMOTION: a per-(agent, scope) grant may auto-approve A2 — EXCEPT ENVOY outbound, which has NO\n8\t    promotion path by construction (SIGIL §4.6). A3 never auto-promotes.\n9\t\n10\tAll state lives on the append-only spine (kill/promotion are events; budget spend is derived from\n11\tthe log), so the governor's every decision is itself auditable — which is what makes the acceptance\n12\tbar (\"zero unauthorized A2/A3 in the audit log\") provable rather than promised. Offense-free by\n13\tdoctrine (assert_no_offense).\"\"\"\n14\tfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\n15\t\n16\tassert_no_offense()\n17\t\n18\tfrom .budget import BudgetCaps, BudgetLedger  # noqa: E402\n19\tfrom .core import Decision, Governor, Outcome  # noqa: E402\n20\tfrom .killswitch import KillSwitch  # noqa: E402\n21\tfrom .promotion import PromotionPolicy  # noqa: E402\n22\t\n23\t__all__ = [\"Governor\", \"Decision\", \"Outcome\", \"KillSwitch\", \"BudgetCaps\", \"BudgetLedger\",\n24\t           \"PromotionPolicy\"]\n25\t"}]},"uuid":"f5636157-6a35-4479-b2e3-94d63bebcda3","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:31:06.294Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6094a080-c62b-435d-a1c3-aa6675b487db","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"dbb8e726-b26e-4601-a36e-6a3c1b51343f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011QJUDHMXXAYoPTtNRerTps","type":"tool_result","content":"sigil/consolidate:\nbrief.py\nextract.py\ngate.py\ngrounding.py\n__init__.py\nmodels.py\npipeline.py\npromote.py\n__pycache__\nqueries.py\nrevise.py\n\nsigil/governor:\nauthn.py\nbudget.py\ncore.py\nidentity.py\n__init__.py\nkillswitch.py\npromotion.py\n\nsigil/graph:\n__init__.py\nquery.py\nrebuild.py\nschema.py\n\nsigil/mcp:\n__init__.py\nserver.py\n===CONFIG===\n\"\"\"SIGIL runtime configuration. All paths local-first, owner-only.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# --- data root (runtime, 0700) --------------------------------------------------------\nSIGIL_HOME = Path(os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_HOME\", str(Path.home() / \".sigil\")))\n\n\ndef _load_env_file() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Load persisted `KEY=VALUE` settings from ~/.sigil/sigil.env so a value set once\n    (e.g. SIGIL_QDRANT_URL for server mode) reaches BOTH the CLI and the MCP server that\n    Claude spawns without an explicit env. The real environment always wins (setdefault).\"\"\"\n    f = SIGIL_HOME / \"sigil.env\"\n    if not f.exists():\n        return\n    for line in f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines():\n        line = line.strip()\n        if not line or line.startswith(\"#\") or \"=\" not in line:\n            continue\n        k, _, v = line.partition(\"=\")\n        os.environ.setdefault(k.strip(), v.strip())\n\n\n_load_env_file()\n\nSPINE_PATH = SIGIL_HOME / \"spine\" / \"spine.jsonl\"\nHEAD_PATH = SIGIL_HOME / \"spine\" / \"head.json\"\nKEYS_DIR = SIGIL_HOME / \"spine\" / \"keys\"\nCACHE_DIR = SIGIL_HOME / \"cache\"\n\n# --- ingestion sources ----------------------------------------------------------------\nCLAUDE_PROJECTS = Path(os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_CLAUDE_PROJECTS\", str(Path.home() / \".claude\" / \"projects\")))\n# Phase-0a thin slice: PENTEST-main only. Slugified cwd = dir name under ~/.claude/projects.\nPROJECT_ALLOWLIST = os.environ.get(\n    \"SIGIL_PROJECT_ALLOWLIST\", \"-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main\"\n).split(\",\")\n# ephemeral CRUCIBLE-engine runs to filter out\nEPHEMERAL_PREFIXES = (\"-tmp-crucible-cc-\", \"-tmp-\")\n\n# --- vectors (Qdrant, local/embedded — no server, no Docker; in-process, file-backed) -\nQDRANT_PATH = SIGIL_HOME / \"qdrant\"                       # embedded local-mode store\nQDRANT_URL = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_QDRANT_URL\", \"\")       # set to switch to a server later\nQDRANT_COLLECTION = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_QDRANT_COLLECTION\", \"sigil_memory\")\nEMBED_MODEL = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_EMBED_MODEL\", \"BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5\")\nEMBED_DIM = 384\n\n# --- graph (Kùzu, deterministic mirror of the spine) ----------------------------------\nGRAPH_DIR = SIGIL_HOME / \"graph\"                          # base; holds current/ + staging/ (atomic swap)\n\n# --- identity / scope -----------------------------------------------------------------\nSCOPE = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_SCOPE\", \"sigil\")          # the owner's single scope\nOWNER_KEY_ID = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_OWNER_KEY_ID\", \"owner\")\n\n\ndef ensure_dirs() -> None:\n    for d in (SIGIL_HOME, SPINE_PATH.parent, KEYS_DIR, CACHE_DIR):\n        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        try:","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"82b47c3e-f8c0-4d20-b341-40982fcde2ae","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:31:10.539Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"dbb8e726-b26e-4601-a36e-6a3c1b51343f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"8e03db07-4a5a-4a35-8c16-0155a155466f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TghR9214D9hH4oNPC7fm8S","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The Governor (SIGIL §5) — the single decision point the agent dispatch gate consults for EVERY\n2\tproposal. It composes the kill switch, budgets, and promotion policy into one fail-closed verdict:\n3\tAUTO (write it), QUEUE (hold for human approval), or DENY (refuse + log). The order matters and is\n4\tdeliberately conservative: observe is always allowed; a kill halts everything above observe; budget\n5\tis checked before any auto-grant; A2 auto-approves only under an explicit promotion; A3 never autos.\n6\t\n7\tKill/promotion are read FRESH per decision (a mid-run kill halts the rest of the run). Backward\n8\tcompatible: with no kill, no cap, and no promotion, A0/A1≤ceiling → AUTO and A2/A3 → QUEUE, exactly\n9\tas the pre-Phase-6 gate behaved.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n13\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n14\tfrom enum import Enum\n15\tfrom typing import Optional\n16\t\n17\tfrom .budget import BudgetCaps, BudgetLedger\n18\tfrom .killswitch import KillSwitch\n19\tfrom .promotion import PromotionPolicy\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tclass Outcome(Enum):\n23\t    AUTO = \"auto\"\n24\t    QUEUE = \"queue\"\n25\t    DENY = \"deny\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n29\tclass Decision:\n30\t    outcome: Outcome\n31\t    reason: str\n32\t\n33\t\n34\tdef _utc_day() -> str:\n35\t    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).date().isoformat()   # runtime bucketing only (never digested)\n36\t\n37\t\n38\tdef _load_caps() -> BudgetCaps:\n39\t    \"\"\"Optional per-agent caps from ~/.sigil/budgets.json ({daily_actions, daily_interrupts}).\n40\t    Absent/malformed → uncapped (budgets opt-in; default behavior unchanged).\"\"\"\n41\t    try:\n42\t        import json\n43\t\n44\t        from ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n45\t        f = SIGIL_HOME / \"budgets.json\"\n46\t        if not f.exists():\n47\t            return BudgetCaps()\n48\t        d = json.loads(f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n49\t        return BudgetCaps(daily_actions=d.get(\"daily_actions\"), daily_interrupts=d.get(\"daily_interrupts\"))\n50\t    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):\n51\t        return BudgetCaps()\n52\t\n53\t\n54\tclass Governor:\n55\t    def __init__(self, store, *, caps: Optional[BudgetCaps] = None, day_iso: Optional[str] = None,\n56\t                 owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n57\t        from .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n58\t        self.store = store\n59\t        ok = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n60\t        tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n61\t        self.kill = KillSwitch(store, owner_key=ok, trusted_pubkey=tp)\n62\t        self.promo = PromotionPolicy(store, owner_key=ok, trusted_pubkey=tp)\n63\t        self.budget = BudgetLedger(store, caps if caps is not None else _load_caps())\n64\t        self._day = day_iso\n65\t\n66\t    def decide(self, *, agent: str, tier, ceiling, scope: Optional[str] = None) -> Decision:\n67\t        from ..agents.base import AUTO_BAR, Tier\n68\t        scope = scope or \"*\"\n69\t\n70\t        # A0 observe/read is ALWAYS allowed — even under a kill switch (perception + memory stay alive).\n71\t        if tier <= Tier.A0:\n72\t            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, \"observe (A0)\")\n73\t\n74\t        # KILL SWITCH: the mesh is halted; nothing above observe may act.\n75\t        if self.kill.is_engaged():\n76\t            return Decision(Outcome.DENY, \"kill switch engaged — agent mesh halted (A0 observe only)\")\n77\t\n78\t        # BUDGET: fail-closed — at cap, deny.\n79\t        over, why = self.budget.over_budget(agent, self._day or _utc_day())\n80\t        if over:\n81\t            return Decision(Outcome.DENY, why)\n82\t\n83\t        # Within the auto bar (A0/A1) and the agent's ceiling → auto, as always.\n84\t        if tier <= AUTO_BAR and tier <= ceiling:\n85\t            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, f\"within auto bar ({tier.label()})\")\n86\t\n87\t        # A2 may auto-approve ONLY under an explicit per-scope promotion (never for ENVOY).\n88\t        if tier == Tier.A2 and tier <= ceiling and self.promo.is_promoted(agent, scope):\n89\t            return Decision(Outcome.AUTO, f\"A2 auto-approved by promotion ({agent}/{scope})\")\n90\t\n91\t        # Everything else (A2 unpromoted, A3, or above ceiling) queues for explicit human approval.\n92\t        return Decision(Outcome.QUEUE, f\"queued for approval ({tier.label()})\")\n93\t"}]},"uuid":"9c99f294-0881-48cf-affc-42957e9e0033","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:31:18.023Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8e03db07-4a5a-4a35-8c16-0155a155466f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"a93b1278-0bf3-4daf-9dce-48a12ed6da87","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014wH1Hi5Vcho5X9QR9yCfbg","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SIGIL's vendored integrity substrate (owner's own CRUCIBLE work, copied verbatim).\n2\t\n3\tThe one seam that would ever couple to the engine — now fully self-contained. SIGIL\n4\timports NO `framework.*` module at all, which is the strongest form of the sovereignty\n5\tdoctrine (§1.3 / §12): the offensive engine cannot even be loaded into a SIGIL process.\n6\t`assert_no_offense()` proves it.\n7\t\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport sys\n11\t\n12\tfrom .canonical import canonical_json, digest_payload, evidence_signing_bytes, sha256_hex\n13\tfrom .chain import append_entry, build_chain, sign_head, verify_chain, verify_head\n14\tfrom .crypto import generate_keypair, sign, verify_one, verify_threshold, KeyPair\n15\tfrom .models import AuthorizerKey, ChainEntry, Signature, SignedChainHead, TrustRoot\n16\t\n17\t__all__ = [\n18\t    \"canonical_json\", \"digest_payload\", \"evidence_signing_bytes\", \"sha256_hex\",\n19\t    \"append_entry\", \"build_chain\", \"sign_head\", \"verify_chain\", \"verify_head\",\n20\t    \"generate_keypair\", \"sign\", \"verify_one\", \"verify_threshold\", \"KeyPair\",\n21\t    \"AuthorizerKey\", \"ChainEntry\", \"Signature\", \"SignedChainHead\", \"TrustRoot\",\n22\t    \"assert_no_offense\",\n23\t]\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tdef assert_no_offense() -> None:\n27\t    \"\"\"Fail-closed: NO framework.* module may be loaded in a SIGIL process (doctrine §12).\"\"\"\n28\t    leaked = [m for m in sys.modules if m == \"framework\" or m.startswith(\"framework.\")]\n29\t    if leaked:\n30\t        raise RuntimeError(f\"SIGIL sovereignty violation: engine modules loaded: {leaked}\")\n31\t"}]},"uuid":"e62757bc-6bd3-482c-ba39-d4025d5739c7","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:31:18.524Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a93b1278-0bf3-4daf-9dce-48a12ed6da87","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"e62757bc-6bd3-482c-ba39-d4025d5739c7","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAA6wpS7vLfJ4FeHtsMc","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Pcu32kspnt9LvSN93poLv5","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /home/kali/sigil && echo \"===mcp server head===\" && head -80 sigil/mcp/server.py && echo \"===consolidate/grounding===\" && cat sigil/consolidate/grounding.py && echo \"===consolidate/models===\" && sed -n '1,60p' sigil/consolidate/models.py","description":"Read MCP server head, grounding, consolidate models"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6050,"cache_read_input_tokens":32437,"output_tokens":389,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6050},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":389,"cache_read_input_tokens":32437,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6050,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6050,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAA6vmSHumLc7WjA5Ljt","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"f4b2092f-9dac-4f35-abae-d21df7bfbfe6","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:31:20.013Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"f4b2092f-9dac-4f35-abae-d21df7bfbfe6","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Pcu32kspnt9LvSN93poLv5","type":"tool_result","content":"===mcp server head===\n\"\"\"SIGIL MCP memory server (Phase 0a: memory.search).\n\nA gated, read-only, loopback stdio server exposing the owner's total recall to any Claude\nsurface. Ports the CRUCIBLE MCP posture (framework/v2/mcp/server.py): a fail-closed tool\nallowlist, no write path, and provenance on every result — each hit is a real, tamper-\nevident spine record (seq + entry_hash) so answers are CITED, and an empty result says so\nrather than inviting fabrication (SIGIL doctrine §1.5, prove-don't-guess).\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom mcp.server.fastmcp import FastMCP\n\nfrom ..config import EMBED_MODEL, QDRANT_URL\nfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\nfrom ..spine.checkpoint import verify_checkpoint\nfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\nfrom ..vectors.index import VectorIndex\n\nassert_no_offense()  # doctrine §12: no engine module may be loaded in a SIGIL process\n\nmcp = FastMCP(\"sigil-memory\")\n_index: VectorIndex | None = None\n\n\ndef _idx() -> VectorIndex:\n    global _index\n    if _index is None:\n        _index = VectorIndex()\n    return _index\n\n\n@mcp.tool()\ndef memory_search(query: str, k: int = 8) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Search the owner's ENTIRE Claude history — everything they've built, decided, or\n    discussed — and return ranked memory records WITH PROVENANCE so you can cite them.\n\n    Each result is a real, tamper-evident spine record: `seq` + `entry_hash` uniquely\n    identify it, `when`/`session`/`project` locate it. When you answer from these, CITE\n    the seq(s). If `results` is empty, memory genuinely has nothing — say so; do NOT\n    fabricate an answer.\n\n    Args:\n        query: what to recall (natural language).\n        k: max results (default 8).\n    \"\"\"\n    hits, is_grounded = _idx().grounded(query, k=max(1, min(int(k), 25)))\n    if not is_grounded:\n        return {\"results\": [],\n                \"note\": \"No strongly-grounded match in SIGIL memory for this query. Do not fabricate — \"\n                        \"tell the owner memory has nothing solid on this (optionally suggest rephrasing).\"}\n    return {\n        \"results\": [\n            {\n                \"seq\": h[\"seq\"], \"kind\": h[\"kind\"], \"when\": h.get(\"ts\"),\n                \"session\": h.get(\"session_id\"), \"project\": h.get(\"project\"),\n                \"entry_hash\": h.get(\"entry_hash\"), \"score\": round(h[\"score\"], 4),\n                \"text\": h.get(\"text\"),\n            }\n            for h in hits\n        ],\n        \"provenance\": \"Each result is a tamper-evident spine record; cite its `seq` (and `entry_hash`) when you answer.\",\n    }\n\n\n@mcp.tool()\ndef episodic_range(start_seq: int = 0, end_seq: int | None = None,\n                   kind: str | None = None, limit: int = 25) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Read a CONTIGUOUS window of the owner's episodic spine in seq order — the raw record\n    stream (messages, tool calls, commits, docs), not a semantic search. Use this to\n    reconstruct *what happened* around a known point (e.g. after `memory_search` gives you a\n    seq, read the seqs around it for context), or to page through a session/day.\n\n    Every record is tamper-evident (seq + entry_hash). Cite seqs when you answer.\n\n    Args:\n        start_seq: first seq to return (inclusive, default 0 = beginning).\n        end_seq: last seq (inclusive); default = newest.\n        kind: optional filter (message | tool_call | tool_result | commit | document | session | decision | commitment | brief).\n===consolidate/grounding===\n\"\"\"Grounding taxonomy + domain-neutral Beta belief math (SIGIL §6.3, ported from\nworldmodel/models.py). Grounding is queryable METADATA that never moves belief; belief is a\nBeta(alpha,beta) whose mean/LCB only ever drop on refutation (demote-only).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport math\n\n# the source stamped on every consolidation-written record (distinguishes it from ingest).\nCONSOLIDATE_SOURCE = \"archivist\"\n\n# grounding tags. A record is a FACT only if its grounding starts with GROUNDED_PREFIX.\nGROUNDED_PREFIX = \"ingest:\"          # traces to real spine records that re-verified\nUNGROUNDED = \"llm:ungrounded\"        # the model asserted it but re-execution failed → commentary\n\n\ndef ground_tag(min_seq: int) -> str:\n    return f\"{GROUNDED_PREFIX}seq={min_seq}\"\n\n\ndef is_grounded(grounding: str | None) -> bool:\n    return bool(grounding) and grounding.startswith(GROUNDED_PREFIX)\n\n\ndef belief_mean(alpha: float, beta: float) -> float:\n    a, b = max(1e-9, alpha), max(1e-9, beta)\n    return a / (a + b)\n\n\ndef belief_lcb(alpha: float, beta: float, z: float = 1.0) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Lower confidence bound = mean − z·sd of Beta(alpha,beta). A wide, low-support belief\n    has a collapsed LCB even when its mean looks acceptable (the consistency.py insight).\"\"\"\n    a, b = max(1e-9, alpha), max(1e-9, beta)\n    mean = a / (a + b)\n    var = (a * b) / ((a + b) ** 2 * (a + b + 1.0))\n    return max(0.0, mean - z * math.sqrt(var))\n===consolidate/models===\n\"\"\"Consolidation domain types (SIGIL §6.3). Offense-free personal-memory analogue of the\nCRUCIBLE recon vocabulary — a candidate fact an extractor proposes, and the gate's verdict.\n\nA `CandidateFact` is a PROPOSAL, never a fact. It becomes a fact only if `gate.admit`\nre-executes its citation successfully; otherwise it is recorded honestly as a refusal. The\nmodel's `model_confidence` can only ever LOWER effective ranking — it never promotes.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass CandidateFact:\n    \"\"\"A candidate durable fact proposed by an ExtractionProvider over spine records.\n\n    `quote` MUST be a verbatim span copied from one of the `source_seqs` records — the gate\n    re-fetches those records and refuses to ground anything whose quote it cannot find.\"\"\"\n    kind: str                       # \"decision\" | \"commitment\" | \"entity\" | \"contradiction\"\n    subject: str                    # canonical subject/key (what this fact is ABOUT)\n    statement: str                  # advisory summary in the owner's terms (NEVER the served fact)\n    quote: str                      # verbatim span from a cited record (the ground)\n    source_seqs: list[int]          # spine seqs the extractor cited\n    model_confidence: float = 0.5   # DEMOTE-ONLY: may lower ranking, never promote\n    owner: str | None = None        # commitments: who is on the hook\n    due_iso: str | None = None      # commitments: due date (ISO), if any\n    extractor: str = \"unknown\"      # provenance of the proposal (heuristic | agent | replay)\n    quotes: tuple[str, ...] = ()    # contradictions: a verbatim quote from EACH conflicting record\n\n    def key_fields(self) -> dict:\n        # identity is the GROUNDED content (the verbatim quote/quotes), not the model's\n        # paraphrase — so two reaffirmations quoting the same span dedupe. Commitments also key\n        # on owner/due (canonicalized) so a CHANGED deadline is a new promotion, not a duplicate.\n        content = \" \".join((self.quote or \"\").split()).casefold()\n        if self.quotes:\n            content = \" || \".join(sorted(\" \".join(q.split()).casefold() for q in self.quotes))\n        base = {\"kind\": self.kind, \"subject\": self.subject.strip().lower(), \"content\": content}\n        if self.kind == \"commitment\":\n            base[\"owner\"] = (self.owner or \"\").strip().casefold()\n            base[\"due_iso\"] = (self.due_iso or \"\").strip()\n        return base\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass GateVerdict:\n    \"\"\"The sole admission authority's decision — decided by RE-EXECUTION, not model text.\"\"\"\n    grounded: bool\n    grounding: str                  # \"ingest:seq=<n>\" (grounded) | \"llm:ungrounded\" (demoted)\n    verified_seqs: list[int] = field(default_factory=list)\n    reason: str = \"\"\n    text: str = \"\"                  # the BYTE-verbatim record span(s) — the served fact content","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"b81f520b-8a65-4980-859c-0d179bd64244","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:31:29.164Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f4b2092f-9dac-4f35-abae-d21df7bfbfe6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"d095b0dd-cb4f-466a-9ad4-019f204c54f8","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YUzEV8izWztYiMC2RgHkr6","type":"tool_result","content":"===egress.py head===\n\"\"\"Frontier-vision EGRESS gate (Phase 7, WS-A V-ii). Sending a screen/camera frame to the frontier\nAnthropic VLM uploads private bytes off the owned machine — that is A2 data-egress, not a free\n\"try local then frontier\" quality bump. This gate makes the cascade doctrine-honest:\n\n  • The tier is DERIVED from the WARDEN oracle (`KernelClassifier` on `vision.frontier.upload` → A2),\n    never self-declared.\n  • Nothing uploads until a VERIFIED owner approval exists that is BOUND to this exact egress — an\n    `egress_token = sha256(frame.sha256 | question)`. An approval of a different egress (a replay)\n    does not match `target_seq` and does not authorize the upload.\n  • Absent approval, the egress is QUEUED (an A2 proposal recorded on the spine) and the frontier\n    reading is WITHHELD; the local (on-box) reading still stands.\n\nReuses the hardened signed `ApprovalQueue` verification (`verify_approval`) — the same fail-closed\nowner-key check the mesh already trusts.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\nfrom ..agents.approvals import verify_approval\nfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n\nEGRESS_SIGNAL = \"vision.egress\"\nFRONTIER_TOOL = \"vision.frontier.upload\"   # classified A2 by the WARDEN oracle (contains \"upload\")\n\n\ndef egress_token(frame_sha256: str, question: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A stable id binding an approval to exactly this (frame, question) upload.\"\"\"\n    return sha256_hex(f\"{frame_sha256}|{question or ''}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n\n\ndef egress_approved(store, seq: int, token: str, trusted_pubkey) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"True iff `seq` is an egress request bound to `token` AND carries a VERIFIED approval by the\n    owner OR an owner-authorized device. Fail-closed: wrong token, no approval, denied, or an\n    approval of a different seq → False.\"\"\"\n    rec = store.get(seq)\n    if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != EGRESS_SIGNAL or rec.payload.get(\"egress_token\") != token:\n        return False\n    from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n    devices = authorized_devices(store, trusted_pubkey)\n    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=seq):\n        p = r.payload\n===vision.py head===\n\"\"\"The VLM seam (SIGIL §8 / C4-C6). A `VisionModel` turns a captured Frame + a question into a\nfree-text visual reading. That reading is ADVISORY — the perception layer serves the frame's\ncaptured OCR TEXT as the authoritative answer and labels the model's reading as unverified (see\n`perceive.compose_perception`). Cascade doctrine: a cheap LOCAL model runs freely (on-box, A0); the\nFRONTIER model is a data-egress hop and is gated (see `perception.egress`).\n\nProviders:\n  • `MoondreamVision` — a local moondream-class VLM via Ollama. Fully offline, zero egress → A0.\n    The default reader. Returns '' on any failure (honest empty, never a fabricated reading).\n  • `ClaudeVision` — the frontier Anthropic vision API. `describe()` performs a REAL image UPLOAD\n    (private screen bytes leave the box), so it MUST only be invoked through `perception.egress`,\n    which classifies the upload A2 via the WARDEN oracle and requires a verified owner approval.\n    Never call `ClaudeVision.describe` on an auto path.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport base64\nimport json\nimport os\nimport urllib.error\nimport urllib.request\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n\nfrom .capture import Frame\n\n\n@runtime_checkable\nclass VisionModel(Protocol):\n    # `egresses` = does describe() send bytes off the box? On-box models (moondream) are False and may\n    # run on the auto A0 path; frontier models are True and may ONLY be invoked through the egress gate\n    # (`Perceptor.frontier`). `perceive`/`ambient_watch` STRUCTURALLY refuse an egressing model.\n    egresses: bool\n    def describe(self, frame: Frame, question: str) -> str: ...   # advisory free-text reading\n\n\ndef _b64_image(frame: Frame) -> Optional[str]:\n    if not frame.image_path:\n        return None\n    try:\n        return base64.b64encode(Path(frame.image_path).read_bytes()).decode(\"ascii\")\n===grep render/fetch in perception===\nsigil/perception/capture.py:7:HONEST GAPS: headless hosts often lack a screenshot tool / tesseract / a camera, so the real\nsigil/perception/capture.py:9:OS accessibility tree (C4); it is not available on a headless box and is left as a documented seam.\"\"\"\nsigil/perception/capture.py:13:import subprocess\nsigil/perception/capture.py:49:        proc = subprocess.run([\"tesseract\", image_path, \"stdout\"],\nsigil/perception/capture.py:52:    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\nsigil/perception/capture.py:59:    on headless hosts.\"\"\"\nsigil/perception/capture.py:65:            proc = subprocess.run([*argv, out], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20)\nsigil/perception/capture.py:66:        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\nsigil/perception/capture.py:80:        proc = subprocess.run(\nsigil/perception/capture.py:83:    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"65d62845-9d7c-4779-a373-33fe7fef97f0","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:31:59.223Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d095b0dd-cb4f-466a-9ad4-019f204c54f8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"1122b5b8-e87e-4adc-900c-d80f933de7de","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012sJKCwwxyirPvVJAJDd6ck","type":"tool_result","content":"160\t### 4.7 STEWARD (personal operations)\n161\t- **Mandate:** run the life layer: calendar, briefs, commitments, recurring admin, finance tracking, health nudges.\n162\t- **Focus:** compounding small wins; the agent you notice least and benefit from most.\n163\t- **Tools:** CalDAV, commitment ledger, brief composer, optional home bridge.\n164\t- **Memory scope:** read full; writes ops records.\n165\t- **Autonomy ceiling:** A2 (calendar writes queued until trust promotes them to A1).\n166\t\n167\t### 4.8 BASTION (defensive security posture)\n168\t- **Mandate:** continuous defensive posture over your own infrastructure: TLS cert expiry, dependency CVE watch, uptime, backup verification, auth-log anomaly flags.\n169\t- **Focus:** your systems only. Explicit doctrine: no scanning of third-party targets, no exploit tooling, no offensive tasking. Requests outside that scope are refused at the agent level and logged.\n170\t- **Tools:** scheduled scanners against an allowlisted asset inventory, CVE feeds, log readers.\n171\t- **Memory scope:** read infra entities; writes findings.\n172\t- **Autonomy ceiling:** A1. Remediation (patching, config changes) is A3.\n173\t\n174\t### 4.9 WARDEN (authorization kernel — subsystem, not an agent)\n175\tEvery tool invocation from every agent passes through WARDEN. See §5.\n176\t\n177\t---\n178\t\n179\t## 5. WARDEN — Autonomy & Authorization Model\n180\t\n181\t### Tiers\n182\t\n183\t| Tier | Class | Examples | Gate |\n184\t|------|-------|----------|------|\n185\t| A0 | Observe / answer | memory queries, screen read, camera frame, voice reply | Auto |\n186\t| A1 | Reversible internal act | write a report to memory, create a brief, open a draft PR branch, raise an alert | Auto + logged |\n187\t| A2 | External-visible / semi-reversible | send email, calendar write, PR ready-for-review, purchase < cap | Queued for one-tap approval; per-scope promotion to auto possible (except ENVOY outbound) |\n188\t| A3 | Destructive / financial / security | push to protected branch, deploy, delete data, spend > cap, infra changes | Explicit approval, per-action, no promotion, signed confirmation |\n189\t\n190\t### Mechanics\n191\t- **Fail-closed:** if tier classification is uncertain, treat as one tier higher.\n192\t- **Signed action spine:** every invocation appends `{ts, agent, tool, args_hash, tier, decision, approver, result_hash, prev_hash}` signed Ed25519. Same construction as your event-spine work; pointed inward.\n193\t- **Budgets:** per-agent daily token/cost caps and interrupt caps enforced by KERNEL.\n194\t- **Kill switch:** single command halts the agent mesh, leaves perception + memory read alive.\n195\t- **Self-audit:** C18 answers \"what did you do and why\" from this log, verbatim.\n196\t\n197\t---\n198\t\n199\t## 6. Memory Substrate"}]},"uuid":"b2df2f84-200a-437c-b7a5-4862200bc551","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:32:10.097Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1122b5b8-e87e-4adc-900c-d80f933de7de","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"e1906a55-9ab2-4aab-a603-93d738fb39f7","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ReSzjajr2StuDSVXxbJPgD","type":"tool_result","content":"308\t---\n309\t\n310\t## 12. Ceiling & Non-Goals\n311\t\n312\tWhat \"most advanced that can exist\" honestly means in 2026:\n313\t\n314\t**In scope and reachable:** always-listening voice-native operation, sight on demand, total recall of your entire working history with provenance, multi-step background execution, proactive situational awareness, a full audit trail of everything it does.\n315\t\n316\t**Out of scope, by reality:** unsupervised long-horizon autonomy over destructive actions (the blocker is error compounding and trust calibration, not model intelligence) and real-time embodied control. honest: claim matches a verbatim quote\n96\t                {\"claim\": \"SIGIL uses Ed25519\", \"source\": src,\n97\t                 \"quote\": \"uses Ed25519 signatures for its action log\", \"confidence\": 0.9},\n98\t                # THE CRITICAL CASE: a FABRICATED claim paired with a REAL (but unrelated) verbatim quote\n99\t                {\"claim\": \"SIGIL stores its private keys in PLAINTEXT on disk\", \"source\": src,\n100\t                 \"quote\": \"uses Ed25519 signatures for its action log\", \"confidence\": 0.95},\n101\t                # a claim whose quote is NOT in the source at all → unverified\n102\t                {\"claim\": \"SIGIL uses RSA-4096\", \"source\": src,\n103\t                 \"quote\": \"RSA-4096 keys everywhere\", \"confidence\": 0.8},\n104\t            ]\n105\t\n106\t    s = _store()\n107\t    res = Scholar(s).run(\"what does SIGIL use for signing?\", [src], synthesizer=_MockSynth())\n108\t    text = s.get(res.applied[0]).payload[\"text\"]\n109\t    # the authoritative served content is the verbatim QUOTE, never the model's free-text claim.\n110\t    assert \"uses Ed25519 signatures for its action log\" in text, \"the verbatim source span is served\"\n111\t    # the fabricated 'plaintext keys' claim must appear ONLY as advisory, never as a verified finding.\n112\t    for line in text.splitlines():\n113\t        if \"plaintext\" in line.lower():\n114\t            assert \"ADVISORY\" in line, \"a fabricated claim must be advisory-only, never source-verified\"\n115\t    assert \"RSA-4096\" in text and \"Unverified\" in text, \"a claim with no verbatim source span is flagged unverified\"\n116\t\n117\t\n118\tdef test_grounds_in_source_verbatim_and_specific():\n119\t    doc = \"the kernel uses Ed25519 signatures and a hash chain\"\n120\t    assert grounds_in_source(\"uses Ed25519 signatures\", doc)\n121\t    assert not grounds_in_source(\"uses RSA keys\", doc), \"a quote absent from the source does not ground\"\n122\t    assert not grounds_in_source(\"the and\", doc), \"a trivial (stopword-only) quote grounds nothing\"\n123\t\n124\t\n125\tif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n126\t    fns = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith(\"test_\")]\n127\t    passed = 0\n128\t    for fn in fns:\n129\t        try:\n130\t            fn(); print(f\"  PASS  {fn.__name__}\"); passed += 1\n131\t        except AssertionError as e:\n132\t            print(f\"  FAIL  {fn.__name__}: {e}\")\n133\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n134\t            print(f\"  ERROR {fn.__name__}: {e}\")\n135\t    print(f\"{passed}/{len(fns)} Phase-4 (ARTIFICER + SCHOLAR) guarantees hold\")\n136\t"}]},"uuid":"080e74be-7cc3-442e-b93c-e3fc790ef798","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:32:31.117Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5cc6d930-5e18-432a-8ed7-58946118c4a3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"add87cb0-36d4-43f7-a482-c10bb89c2929","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018M9YqUuYLWVwtndb4SUY6p","type":"tool_result","content":"140\t    # POSITIVE allowlist (not a name-substring blocklist): any NEW public method — regardless of name —\n141\t    # fails this until a reviewer adds it here, so offensive-capability drift can't slip in unnamed (RP-4).\n142\t    public = {n for n in dir(Bastion) if not n.startswith(\"_\")}\n143\t    allowed = {\"run\", \"probe_target\", \"name\", \"mandate\", \"ceiling\"}\n144\t    assert public == allowed, f\"unreviewed public surface on BASTION (capability drift?): {public ^ allowed}\"\n145\t\n146\t\n147\tdef test_bastion_uptime_never_follows_redirect_offscope():\n148\t    # RP-BASTION-01: the REAL UrllibUptimeSource must not follow a 3xx to a non-allowlisted host.\n149\t    import http.server\n150\t    import threading\n151\t    hits = {\"off\": 0}\n152\t\n153\t    class Off(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\n154\t        def do_HEAD(self): hits[\"off\"] += 1; self.send_response(200); self.end_headers()\n155\t        def log_message(self, *a): pass\n156\t\n157\t    class Allow(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\n158\t        def do_HEAD(self):\n159\t            self.send_response(302)\n160\t            self.send_header(\"Location\", f\"http://127.0.0.1:{off_port}/pwned\")\n161\t            self.end_headers()\n162\t        def log_message(self, *a): pass\n163\t\n164\t    off = http.server.HTTPServer((\"127.0.0.1\", 0), Off)\n165\t    off_port = off.server_address[1]\n166\t    allow = http.server.HTTPServer((\"127.0.0.1\", 0), Allow)\n167\t    allow_ref = f\"http://127.0.0.1:{allow.server_address[1]}/health\"\n168\t    for srv in (off, allow):\n169\t        threading.Thread(target=srv.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()\n170\t    try:\n171\t        up, status = UrllibUptimeSource(timeout=5).probe(allow_ref)\n172\t    finally:\n173\t        off.shutdown(); allow.shutdown()\n174\t    assert hits[\"off\"] == 0, \"the real uptime probe must NEVER follow a redirect to an off-scope host\"\n175\t    assert status == 302 and up is True, \"the 3xx is the reported status; the hop is refused\"\n176\t\n177\t\n178\tdef test_bastion_uptime_down_fires_grounded():\n179\t    store = _store()\n180\t    b = Bastion(store, inventory=[Asset(\"api\", \"uptime\", \"https://api.local/health\")],\n181\t                uptime_source=DictUptimeSource({\"https://api.local/health\": (False, 503)}))\n182\t    res = b.run()\n183\t    assert len(res.applied) == 1 and b.store.get(res.applied[0]).payload[\"severity\"] == \"high\"\n184\t    assert \"status=503\" in b.store.get(res.applied[0]).payload[\"quote\"]\n185\t\n186\t\n187\t# ---- ACCEPTANCE: BASTION findings surface in the morning brief ------------------------------------\n188\tdef test_bastion_findings_surface_in_brief():\n189\t    store = _store()"}]},"uuid":"d021d396-6778-479a-9820-8e3c94ca6314","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:32:41.561Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"add87cb0-36d4-43f7-a482-c10bb89c2929","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"595af93b-471f-4d64-a0d1-28f02e0d9ade","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016UEfjrncwQqoJU15eoaJej","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The mesh registry (Phase 7, WS-D). Two owner-signed ledgers on the one spine:\n2\t\n3\t  • host_capability — each host advertises {os, screen, camera, gpu_vlm, always_on}, owner-signed, so\n4\t    an agent can route work to the best-capable ONLINE host from cited, tamper-evident records.\n5\t  • device authorization — a 1-of-N device ledger: the owner AUTHORIZES a phone's own Ed25519 device\n6\t    key with a signed `device_authorized` record (and `device_revoked` to remove it). The phone then\n7\t    approves A2/A3 items by signing with ITS key; `verify_approval(..., extra_pubkeys=authorized)`\n8\t    accepts it. Trust is pinned to owner-MINTED keys (RP-APPROVAL-2); the phone never holds the\n9\t    trust-root key, and can approve offline.\n10\t\n11\tAll reads are fail-closed: a descriptor/authorization that does not verify against the owner key is\n12\tignored. Reuses `governor.authn` (the same signed-event primitive kill/promotion/approval use).\"\"\"\n13\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n14\t\n15\tfrom typing import List, Optional, Set\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n18\tfrom ..agents.approvals import _approval_message\n19\tfrom ..governor.authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n20\tfrom ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n21\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex, sign\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tCAP_SIGNAL = \"mesh.host_capability\"\n25\tDEV_SIGNAL = \"mesh.device\"\n26\t_CAP_CORE = (\"signal\", \"host_id\", \"os\", \"has_screen\", \"has_camera\", \"has_gpu_vlm\", \"always_on\")\n27\t_DEV_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\", \"device_id\", \"device_pubkey\")\n28\t\n29\t\n30\t# --- host capability advertisement -----------------------------------------------------------------\n31\tdef advertise_capability(store: SpineStore, descriptor: dict, owner_key) -> int:\n32\t    core = {\"signal\": CAP_SIGNAL, \"host_id\": descriptor[\"host_id\"], \"os\": descriptor[\"os\"],\n33\t            \"has_screen\": bool(descriptor[\"has_screen\"]), \"has_camera\": bool(descriptor[\"has_camera\"]),\n34\t            \"has_gpu_vlm\": bool(descriptor[\"has_gpu_vlm\"]), \"always_on\": bool(descriptor[\"always_on\"])}\n35\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n36\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n37\t\n38\t\n39\tdef capability_map(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> dict:\n40\t    \"\"\"Latest VERIFIED capability per host_id (forged/unsigned advertisements ignored).\"\"\"\n41\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n42\t    latest: dict = {}\n43\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n44\t        p = r.payload\n45\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == CAP_SIGNAL and verify_signed(p, _CAP_CORE, tp):\n46\t            latest[p.get(\"host_id\")] = {k: p.get(k) for k in _CAP_CORE if k != \"signal\"}\n47\t    return latest\n48\t\n49\t\n50\t# --- device authorization ledger -------------------------------------------------------------------\n51\tdef authorize_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n52\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"authorized\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n53\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n54\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n55\t\n56\t\n57\tdef revoke_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n58\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"revoked\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n59\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n60\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tdef authorized_devices(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> Set[str]:\n64\t    \"\"\"The set of currently-authorized device pubkeys (owner-signed authorize, minus later revoke).\n65\t    An unsigned/forged authorization is ignored — a rogue device cannot self-authorize.\"\"\"\n66\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n67\t    state: dict = {}\n68\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n69\t        p = r.payload\n70\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == DEV_SIGNAL and p.get(\"state\") in (\"authorized\", \"revoked\") \\\n71\t                and verify_signed(p, _DEV_CORE, tp):\n72\t            state[p.get(\"device_pubkey\")] = p[\"state\"]      # latest verified state per device key\n73\t    return {pub for pub, st in state.items() if st == \"authorized\"}\n74\t\n75\t\n76\tclass DeviceApprover:\n77\t    \"\"\"Signs an approval with a DEVICE key (the phone). The record verifies via\n78\t    `verify_approval(record, owner_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(store))` — i.e. only while\n79\t    the device remains authorized. Never touches the owner trust-root key.\"\"\"\n80\t    def __init__(self, store: SpineStore, *, device_key):\n81\t        self.store = store\n82\t        self.device_key = device_key\n83\t\n84\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, approver: str, reason: str) -> int:\n85\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n86\t        sig = sign(self.device_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n87\t        payload = {\"signal\": _APPROVAL_SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq,\n88\t                   \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason, \"pubkey\": self.device_key.public_key_b64,\n89\t                   \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg), \"device\": True, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n90\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\", payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n91\t\n92\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n93\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver, reason)\n94\t\n95\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n96\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver, reason)\n97\t"}]},"uuid":"78fea704-5bb7-4062-908a-5f52a52caf5a","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:32:56.275Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"595af93b-471f-4d64-a0d1-28f02e0d9ade","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"59cf579f-8162-4b76-85e6-2eb4be6a402f","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"aacad8fd30a6e76c0","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAAEmSpkVVCpHphiQQYL","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I have gathered enough of the substrate to design against the real code. I verified every reuse point named in the brief (SSRF-gated `read_source` + pinning + `_NoRedirect`, `grounds_in_source`, `gate.admit`/`_real_span`/`salient`, `OperatorScope`, `BudgetLedger`, `SpineStore.append` + `KINDS`, `tiers.rs` fail-closed classification, the `VisionModel` provider/egress pattern, and the local-HTTP-server test idiom in `test_perception_bastion.py`).\n\nNote on output: per the READ-ONLY doctrine that supersedes other instructions, I did not write a plan file. The full design is below as my response.\n\n---\n\n# SIGIL Web-Research Engine (\"SCRIBE\") — Peak-Innovative Grounded Scraper: Design & Plan\n\nA future-grade grounded research engine that crawls **public** content, grounds **every** served fact to a verbatim source span cited to the spine, respects robots.txt + per-host rate limits, scope-gates its reach to owner-authorized domains, and feeds cited knowledge into the memory spine. Built reuse-first on the existing SSRF gate, veracity gate, spine, and governor.\n\n---\n\n## 0. Anchoring facts confirmed in the substrate (reuse points, verbatim)\n\n- **Fetch:** `sigil/sigil/agents/sources.py` — `read_source()` resolves the host once (`_vetted_ip`), verifies every resolved address is public-unicast, **pins** the IP (`_PinnedHTTPConnection`/`_PinnedHTTPSConnection`, SNI+cert stay on the original host), refuses all 3xx (`_NoRedirect`), strips HTML (`_strip_html`), never raises → `\"\"`. Stable UA `SIGIL-SCHOLAR/1.0 (authorized owner research)`. Confirmed gap: **no** robots, **no** rate-limit, **no** status surfacing (urllib `HTTPError` on 4xx/5xx is swallowed to `\"\"`), and `_strip_html` + `_WS.sub(\" \")` **collapses whitespace** (destroys the line structure robots.txt needs).\n- **Grounding:** `agents/scholar.py::grounds_in_source(quote, text)` = verbatim substring AND ≥2 salient tokens; `compose_report` serves the verbatim span, model claim advisory-only; `Synthesizer` Protocol + `ClaudeSynthesizer` + deterministic double. **Stronger reuse available:** `consolidate/gate.py::admit(cand, window_seqs, store)` re-fetches each cited spine record, grounds the verbatim quote, enforces cited-seq ∈ window (fabricated-citation guard), and returns the **byte-verbatim served span** (`_real_span`) in `verdict.text`. `salient()` is the shared tokenizer.\n- **Scope model:** `agents/operator_scope.py::OperatorScope` — realpath allowlist, empty = deny-all, returns the vetted object so the caller uses exactly what was checked (no TOCTOU). This is the template for a host/domain allowlist.\n- **Throttle/budget:** `governor/budget.py::BudgetLedger`/`BudgetCaps` — coarse per-agent per-UTC-day record cap, opt-in `~/.sigil/budgets.json`, fail-closed in `Governor.decide`. Confirmed: **no per-host request-rate limiter exists** — must be built.\n- **Spine feed:** `spine/store.py::append(kind, source, actor, payload, parent_id, supersedes_id)` hash-chained; `spine/models.py::KINDS` frozenset (has `document`, `report`; a `web_page` add is a one-line reviewed change); `Agent._dispatch` routes every proposal through `Governor.decide` (kill/budget/promotion) and writes the outcome. `consolidate/promote.py` promotes grounded facts to entities/graph.\n- **Warden:** `kernel/src/tiers.rs::classify` token-based, danger-first, fail-closed to A3. Confirmed: `http.get`→A0 (`get` is an A0 verb); `web.fetch`/`scrape.fetch`/`crawl.fetch`→**A3** (no positive classification → fail-closed). SCHOLAR ceiling A1.\n- **Provider + egress pattern to mirror:** `perception/vision.py` (`VisionModel` Protocol, local-vs-frontier, `egresses` flag, advisory reading) + `perception/egress.py` (a data-egress hop is tier-derived from the Warden oracle and requires a bound, verified owner approval).\n- **Deterministic local-server test idiom:** `tests/test_perception_bastion.py::test_bastion_uptime_never_follows_redirect_offscope` (threaded `http.server` on `127.0.0.1:0`, hit counters, redirect-to-off-host). `tests/test_hardening.py` SSRF + IP-pin tests to extend.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Peak-innovative mechanisms\n\n### (a) Bounded crawl frontier — VOI-priority BFS, scope-gated\n\n- **`ScrapeScope`** (new, mirrors `OperatorScope`): an allowlist of registrable domains / exact hosts. A candidate URL is admitted iff **(1)** scheme ∈ {http,https}, **(2)** host passes `is_public_host` (the SSRF gate, reused), **(3)** the IDNA-encoded, lowercased, port-normalized host matches the allowlist (exact-host by default; opt-in eTLD+1 to include subdomains). **Empty allowlist = deny-all** (fail-closed, exactly `OperatorScope`'s inversion of the unguarded default). Returns the normalized host actually vetted so the fetch keys the rate-limiter on the same string (no check/use drift).\n- **`Frontier`**: a max-heap priority queue of `(score, url, depth, parent_seq)` with hard caps `max_pages`, `max_depth`, `max_per_host`, and a normalized-URL `seen` set (strip fragment, sort query, lowercase host) to kill cycles/dupes.\n  - **VOI score (value-of-information):** rank a candidate by salient-token overlap between the research question and the candidate's **anchor text + URL path tokens** (reuse `consolidate/gate.py::salient`), minus a depth penalty, minus a per-host saturation penalty. This spends the page budget on the pages most likely to *ground* an answer rather than blind BFS — the \"way more\" is smarter allocation, not more reach.\n- **Per fetch** reuses the SSRF-gated pinned fetch (new `fetch_raw`, slice 1) → static HTML, then extraction (c). Every hop — page, robots, and any render — passes `is_public_host` + `ScrapeScope` + robots + rate-limit **before** a socket opens.\n\n### (b) Politeness layer — real per-host limiter + robots RESPECT + backoff\n\n- **`RateLimiter`** (new, the missing primitive): per-host **min-interval** core (map `host → last_fetch_monotonic`; `acquire(host)` computes/serializes the wait), with an optional token-bucket burst. **Injectable `clock`+`sleep`** so tests assert the computed wait deterministically without real sleeping. Per-host serialized (concurrency 1/host). The rate key is the **normalized host** from `ScrapeScope` so two spellings (case/port/IDNA/confusable) can't get two buckets.\n- **`robots.py`**: fetch `/robots.txt` via the **SSRF-gated `fetch_raw`** (NOT `RobotFileParser.read()`, which would make its own unpinned, un-vetted request — an SSRF hole), then `RobotFileParser.parse(lines)` (stdlib, no new dep). Expose `can_fetch(UA, url)` + `crawl_delay`/`request_rate`. **Doctrine = respect, never evade:** a Disallowed URL is **dropped and recorded as a skip**, never fetched. The site's `crawl-delay` feeds the limiter as `max(our_min, site_delay)`. Per-host cached for the run. **Fail-closed on ambiguity:** 404/empty robots → allowed (standard); **5xx/timeout robots → treat as disallow-all for that host this run** (polite + safe).\n- **Backoff:** `fetch_raw` **surfaces the HTTP status** (fixing today's swallow-to-`\"\"`). On **429/503**, honor `Retry-After`, else capped exponential backoff with a max-retries ceiling; on exhaustion drop the URL (honest empty). The correlatable UA is reused unchanged — **no rotation**.\n\n### (c) Structured extraction — beyond HTML-strip\n\n- **`extract.py`** (stdlib `html.parser`, dependency-free to match `sources.py`): `links(base_url, html)` (relative→absolute via `urljoin`, with anchor text for VOI), `tables(html)` (row/col structure), `main_text(html)` (reuse `_strip_html` baseline, optionally drop `<nav>/<footer>` boilerplate), `entities(text)` (reuse `salient()` + capitalized-run heuristic). **Discipline:** tables/links/entities are **navigation + advisory metadata**; they are never served as grounded facts unless a verbatim quote grounds them through the gate.\n- **`render.py`** — the WS-F headless-browser seam, mirroring `VisionModel`: a `RenderProvider` Protocol, default **`NullRenderer`** (returns `\"\"` — honest gap on headless boxes), optional **`HeadlessRenderer`** invoked **only** when static text is near-empty (a JS-app heuristic). **This is a data-egress + attack-surface hop** (a browser follows redirects, loads third-party subresources, runs attacker JS — bypassing the pinned-IP + no-redirect SSRF gate), so it is **off by default, owner-opt-in per-scope** (mirroring `perception/egress.py`'s bound-approval gate), re-vets the host with `is_public_host`, runs cookieless/credential-free, and blocks private-IP subresource loads; if the shared provider can't enforce private-IP blocking, the renderer **stays a documented, disabled seam**.\n\n### (d) Grounded research orchestration — reuse the veracity gate wholesale\n\nThe peak move: scraped claims pass through the **identical demote-only admission choke point** as consolidation.\n\n1. Frontier crawl → pages (each already SSRF+robots+rate gated).\n2. **Persist each fetched page** as a spine record (`web_page`, source `web`, actor `SCHOLAR`) capturing `{url, fetched_at, content_hash=sha256(raw), ua, robots_allowed, http_status, text}` where `text` = **exactly the text handed to the synthesizer** (same truncation window) so what the model saw == what the gate re-checks. Collect these seqs = `window_seqs`.\n3. Synthesize claims (reuse `ClaudeSynthesizer` / deterministic double) → `CandidateFact`s citing the page-record **seq(s)** + a verbatim quote.\n4. **`gate.admit(cand, window_seqs, store)`** (reused verbatim): re-fetches the cited record, grounds the verbatim quote, rejects any seq outside the fetched window (fabricated-citation guard), returns the **byte-verbatim served span**. Grounded → served span; ungrounded → recorded honestly as advisory, never dropped.\n5. Compose a cited **`report`** (A1) via `Agent._dispatch` → `Governor.decide` (kill/budget/promotion enforced) → spine. The served content is the **verbatim span**; the model's claim is advisory (exactly `compose_report`'s discipline, now citing spine seqs).\n6. Optional: feed grounded entities into `consolidate/promote.py` for graph promotion (`cites`/`monitors` edges), so scraped knowledge is recallable via the existing `memory_search`/MCP tools with full provenance.\n\n### (e) The orchestrator extending SCHOLAR\n\n`WebResearcher` (subclass of `Scholar`, same `name=\"SCHOLAR\"`, ceiling **A1**, so budgets/kill/mesh wiring apply unchanged) with a `research_web(question, seeds, scope)` entrypoint: fan out over public sources via the frontier, ground every claim through the gate, synthesize one cited report into memory. Mirrors the deep-research pattern (fan-out → fetch → adversarially verify → synthesize cited report) — but every \"verify\" is the byte-verbatim gate, not model self-assessment.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Test + real-proof strategy\n\n**Deterministic local test site** (threaded `http.server` on `127.0.0.1:0`, hit counters, per the `test_perception_bastion.py` idiom), serving:\n- `/index.html` — links to `/page-a`, `/page-b`, `/private` (robots-disallowed), and an **off-scope absolute link** to a second server port.\n- `/page-a.html` — a known verbatim sentence (grounding target).\n- `/page-b.html` — a `<table>` + a second known quote.\n- `/robots.txt` — `Disallow: /private`, `Crawl-delay: 1`.\n- `/slow` — `503` + `Retry-After` (backoff).\n- `/rebind` — `302` to a loopback URL (no-redirect + SSRF).\n\n**Assertions:**\n- **Robots respected:** `/private` hit-count stays **0**; a Disallow after relative-link resolution is still honored; 5xx robots ⇒ host disallowed.\n- **Per-host rate limiter enforces min-interval:** with an injected clock, assert `acquire` computes the correct wait and per-host isolation; two URL spellings share one bucket; `crawl-delay` raises the interval.\n- **SSRF refusal:** a loopback/private seed and a `169.254.169.254`/`127.0.0.1` link are refused *before any socket*; extend the existing IP-pin spy test to the frontier + robots + render paths (assert `socket.create_connection` only ever sees the vetted public IP).\n- **Every served claim verbatim-grounded + cited:** over the local site, each served span is a byte-substring of a fetched page and cites a real spine seq; a **fabricated** claim (mock synth pairing a false claim with a real-but-unrelated quote — the `test_hardening` critical case) appears **advisory-only**; an ungrounded claim is demoted, not served.\n- **Scope-gate:** the off-scope absolute link is never fetched (hit-count 0).\n- **Budget/kill:** with a `daily_actions` cap the Nth report is DENIED and logged; kill switch halts crawling above A0.\n- **Real public-fetch proof (skip-if-offline):** one real GET to a stable public URL (e.g. `example.com`) via `read_source`; skip if unreachable (existing skip idiom); assert a known verbatim span grounds.\n\n**Red-pen lenses** (run the `adversarial-sweep` skill as the RED-PEN companion before merge):\n1. **SSRF/rebinding through the frontier** — every hop (page, robots, render) vetted+pinned; assert `RobotFileParser.read()` is never called and the renderer re-vets.\n2. **Robots ignored** — Disallow honored on all paths incl. via redirects/relative links; cache can't be poisoned to allow-all; 5xx fails closed.\n3. **Rate-limit bypass** — many-host fan-out and URL-spelling variants can't split a host's bucket; global page/host/depth caps hold.\n4. **Fabricated/ungrounded fact served** — `gate.admit` demote-only; served content is the verbatim span; `window_seqs` blocks citing un-fetched pages.\n5. **Scope-gate escape** — off-allowlist via redirect (blocked), relative-link normalization, IDNA/unicode confusables, `userinfo@host`, IP-literal host.\n6. **Scraper-as-recon/attack** — GET-only, no forms/login/non-GET/fuzzing/port-scan; a positive-surface lock test (mirror BASTION's `test_bastion_public_surface_is_allowlisted`) asserts the `scrape` package exposes **no** method that submits a body / non-GET / auth.\n7. **PII/credential harvesting** — no auth, no cookies, no paywalled/gated fetches; harvested data stays local (spine 0700), never re-egressed.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Doctrine-safety analysis (CLAUDE.md §II/§VI · SIGIL.md §4.8/§12)\n\n- **Public-only:** `is_public_host`/`_vetted_ip`/IP-pinning on **every** hop + `ScrapeScope` allowlist + no auth/cookies ⇒ only anonymously-public content ever leaves the box or enters memory.\n- **Correlatable, not evasive:** the single stable UA is reused unchanged; **no** UA/IP rotation, **no** CAPTCHA-solving, **no** robots evasion, **no** header spoofing. The crawler is identifiable and honors the site's rules — the explicit anti-evasion posture of §4.8.\n- **Throttled:** per-host min-interval + `crawl-delay` respect + 429/503 backoff + page/host/depth caps + `BudgetLedger` daily caps + kill switch.\n- **Scope-gated:** `ScrapeScope` deny-all-empty; the owner authorizes each domain explicitly, exactly like `OperatorScope`.\n- **Serve-the-quote:** `gate.admit`/`grounds_in_source` — the verbatim span is the served fact, the model's paraphrase is advisory, ungrounded claims demoted (never dropped, never asserted).\n- **Never-attack:** GET-only, no forms/login/injection/fuzzing/port-scan; renderer sandboxed + off-by-default; `assert_no_offense()` already forbids loading any `framework.*` engine module into a SIGIL process.\n- **Warden-honest tiering:** the crawl read is an A0 observe (read-only, no external state change — like `read_source` and BASTION's uptime probe today); the only classified write is the A1 `report`, within SCHOLAR's ceiling. The **fail-closed A3** of `scrape.fetch`/`web.fetch` is retained *as a guard*: no generic \"scrape\" verb can auto-run on the mesh/MCP bus (slice-0 lock proves it).\n\n**Abuse vectors a reviewer will hunt:** SSRF via the robots.txt fetch; redirect/relative/IDNA-based scope escape; unbounded (\"billion-page\") frontier ⇒ caps; third-party DoS/amplification ⇒ per-host serialize + backoff + caps; storing a third party's private data ⇒ public-only + local-only; the headless browser as an SSRF/exfil/attack surface ⇒ off-by-default + re-vet + sandbox.\n\n---\n\n## 5. How \"way more\" stays offense-free — the NOT list\n\nThe engine will **NOT**: submit forms; log in or carry cookies/credentials/tokens; send POST/PUT/DELETE or any non-GET; solve CAPTCHAs; rotate UA or IP; evade or ignore robots.txt or rate limits; fuzz paths; port-scan or probe for vulnerabilities; brute-force; follow redirects to non-allowlisted or internal hosts; fetch internal/private/loopback hosts; scrape paywalled/auth-gated content; run attacker JS with any credential/cookie; re-egress harvested data (spine is local, 0700); or auto-act on findings (research is A0/A1 — any external action stays A2/A3 gated by design).\n\n**\"Way more\" = depth in epistemics, not reach in offense:** more grounding (byte-verbatim gate), more provenance (every claim cites a tamper-evident page record), more structure (tables/entities/graph promotion), more recall (cited knowledge in the spine), smarter allocation (VOI frontier).
